{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"09338746-ab51-40b7-a30b-3e797eb69648","agentId":"a94df24a264e8dc9c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"你是资深代码审计专家。对 Orion 平台的 **AI 平台 (/ai)** 模块进行深度能力评估，包含 ChatOps 专项分析。\n\n【铁律】\n1. 质量 > 数量。每条结论必须附带 file:line 证据\n2. 禁止仅通过文件名推断功能，必须读取代码确认\n3. 看到 setTimeout/硬编码数组/注释掉的不算实现\n4. 每条 API 调用必须追踪到后端路由+Controller+Service+Repository\n\n【必须完成的 6 个任务】\n1. 路由注册检查：routes.ts 是否 import 并 register？未注册 = 404\n2. 完整调用链追踪：按钮 → handle → API → 路由 → Controller → Service → Repository → DB，每步标注 ✅/❌/⚠️\n3. 代码级验证：setTimeout=Mock、硬编码=Mock、catch降级、Map=非持久化\n4. 4 维度评分：代码完整度/持久化/API对接/交互完整\n5. 场景逆向验证：选一个真实流程走完，记录断在哪个环节\n6. ChatOps 专项评估（见下方标准）\n7. 输出缺失能力清单（P0/P1/P2 分级）\n\n【ChatOps 专项评估标准】\n| 能力项 | 评估标准 | 对标 |\n|--------|---------|------|\n| Bot 能力 | 多平台接入（钉钉/飞书/企微/Slack） | 覆盖面 |\n| 命令管理 | 命令注册/版本/权限控制 | 规范性 |\n| 工作流执行 | 通过聊天触发 Pipeline/部署/审批 | 核心能力 |\n| 审批处理 | 聊天中完成审批 + 二次确认 | 安全性 |\n| 告警响应 | 告警推送 → 聊天处理 → 自动恢复 | 闭环 |\n| 智能推荐 | 基于上下文推荐操作/命令 | 智能化 |\n| 权限控制 | 基于角色的命令执行权限 | 安全性 |\n| 审计日志 | 所有聊天操作记录可追溯 | 合规性 |\n| 集成能力 | 与 CI/CD/监控/工单深度集成 | 生态力 |\n| 多轮对话 | 上下文保持 + 意图理解 | 体验 |\n\n【输出格式】\n```markdown\n# AI 平台 (/ai) 深度能力评估报告（含 ChatOps 专项）\n\n## 一、模块概览\n\n## 二、交互完整度\n\n## 三、样式规范符合度\n\n## 四、逻辑完整度（含调用链追踪）\n\n## 五、功能缺失识别\n\n## 六、微前端拆分评估\n\n## 七、ChatOps 专项评估\n| 能力项 | 状态(✅❌⚠️) | 评分(1-10) | 证据(file:line) | 对标行业标杆 |\n\n## 八、场景逆向验证\n场景：\"[描述]\"\n步骤 1-7...\n\n## 九、缺失能力清单\n### P0\n### P1\n### P2\n```\n\n【工作目录】/Users/heal/orion-design\n\n开始执行。先列出要扫描的文件清单，然后逐页深度分析。"},"uuid":"3a5ef069-401d-46df-a849-b2459903116f","timestamp":"2026-05-22T07:56:14.335Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}

new RLSPolicyManager(database);\n215\t  }\n216\t\n217\t  return { isolationService, rlsPolicyManager };\n218\t}\n219\t\n220\t/**\n221\t * 为路由模块注册带 JWT 认证 + 角色校验的封装插件。\n222\t *\n223\t * Fastify 的插件封装机制确保 addHook 注册的 onRequest 钩子\n224\t * 仅作用于该插件内部注册的路由，不影响其他路由。\n225\t *\n226\t * @param app - Fastify 实例\n227\t * @param routeModule - 路由模块函数\n228\t * @param prefix - 路由前缀（如 '/users'）\n229\t * @param routeOptions - 传递给路由模块的自定义选项（不含 prefix）\n230\t * @param requiredRoles - 所需角色列表\n231\t */\n232\tasync function registerWithRoleGuard(\n233\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n234\t  routeModule: (instance: FastifyInstance, opts?: any) => Promise<void>,\n235\t  prefix: string,\n236\t  routeOptions?: Record<string, unknown>,\n237\t  _requiredRoles: readonly string[] = ADMIN_ROLES\n238\t): Promise<void> {\n239\t  // 已迁移到 registerWithPermission：各路由内部使用 requirePermission 进行细粒度权限控制\n240\t  return registerWithPermission(app, routeModule, prefix, routeOptions);\n241\t}\n242\t\n243\t/**\n244\t * 使用 requirePermission 的模块级注册（细粒度权限控制）\n245\t * @param routeModule - 路由模块函数\n246\t * @param prefix - 路由前缀\n247\t * @param routeOptions - 传递给路由模块的选项\n248\t * @param resourceType - 资源类型（如 'user', 'pipeline'）\n249\t * @param defaultAction - 默认操作（如 'read', 'write'）\n250\t */\n251\tasync function registerWithPermission(\n252\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n253\t  routeModule: (instance: FastifyInstance, opts?: any) => Promise<void>,\n254\t  prefix: string,\n255\t  routeOptions?: Record<string, unknown>,\n256\t  resourceType?: string,\n257\t  defaultAction?: string\n258\t): Promise<void> {\n259\t  await app.register(async (instance: FastifyInstance) => {\n260\t    // Skip auth in development for easier testing\n261\t    const isDev = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development';\n262\t    if (!isDev) {\n263\t      instance.addHook('onRequest', authenticateUser);\n264\t    }\n265\t    // 具体的 requirePermission 在各路由内部添加\n266\t    await instance.register(routeModule, { prefix, ...routeOptions });\n267\t  });\n268\t}\n269\t\n270\texport default async function apiRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, options: ApiRoutesOptions): Promise<void> {\n271\t  // ==================== SSE Service Initialization (shared with pipeline engine) ====================\n272\t  const pipelineLogSSE = new PipelineLogSSEService(new EventEmitter());\n273\t\n274\t  // ==================== LLM Trace Service Initialization ====================\n275\t  initLLMTrace(options.database);\n276\t\n277\t  // ==================== 租户隔离服务初始化 ====================\n278\t  // 初始化四层租户隔离服务 (P0 Task 6)\n279\t  const { isolationService, rlsPolicyManager } = initializeTenantIsolation(options.database);\n280\t\n281\t  // 注册全局租户验证中间件\n282\t  if (options.enableTenantIsolation !== false) {\n283\t    // Layer 1: API层 - TenantValidatorMiddleware\n284\t    const tenantValidatorMiddleware = createTenantValidatorMiddleware(isolationService, {\n285\t      required: true,\n286\t      skipPaths: ['/healthz', '/readyz', '/version', '/api/v1/info', '/api/v1/public', '/metrics'],\n287\t    });\n288\t\n289\t    app.addHook('onRequest', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n290\t      return tenantValidatorMiddleware(request, reply, () => {});\n291\t    });\n292\t\n293\t    // Layer 4: Database RLS — 请求级连接 + AsyncLocalStorage\n294\t    // 替代原有的 pool.query() 方式，确保每个请求使用专用连接，\n295\t    // RLS session variable 在同一连接上设置和查询。\n296\t    if (options.database && rlsPolicyManager) {\n297\t      app.addHook('preHandler', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n298\t        const tenantCtx = (request as any).tenantContext;\n299\t        if (!tenantCtx) return;\n300\t        const tenant = tenantCtx.getCurrentTenant();\n301\t        if (!tenant) return;\n302\t\n303\t        // 从连接池获取专用连接\n304\t        const client = await options.database!.getConnection();\n305\t\n306\t        try {\n307\t          // 在该连接上设置 RLS session variable（SET SESSION 级别）\n308\t          await client.query(\n309\t            \"SELECT set_config('app.current_tenant_id', $1, false), set_config('app.tenant_isolation', $2, false)\",\n310\t            [String(tenant.tenantId), 'true']\n311\t          );\n312\t\n313\t          // 将 client 挂载到 request 上，供 ALS enterWith 使用\n314\t          (request as any).dbClient = client;\n315\t\n316\t          // 进入 ALS 上下文，后续所有 async 调用（包括 handler、service、repository）\n317\t          // 的 DatabasePool.query() 都会自动使用此 client\n318\t          tenantContextStorage.enterWith({\n319\t            dbClient: client,\n320\t            tenantId: tenant.tenantId,\n321\t          });\n322\t        } catch (error) {\n323\t          // set_config 失败 → 拒绝请求（安全失败模式）\n324\t          try {\n325\t            client.release();\n326\t          } catch {}\n327\t          reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Tenant isolation setup failed' });\n328\t          throw new Error('Failed to set tenant context');\n329\t        }\n330\t      });\n331\t\n332\t      // 成功响应时清理连接\n333\t      app.addHook('onResponse', async (request: FastifyRequest) => {\n334\t        const client = (request as any).dbClient as PoolClient | undefined;\n335\t        if (client) {\n336\t          try {\n337\t            // 先 ROLLBACK（幂等，无活跃事务时无影响），确保连接不在 aborted 状态\n338\t            await client.query('ROLLBACK').catch(() => {});\n339\t            // 清除 session 变量\n340\t            await client.query(\n341\t              \"SELECT set_config('app.current_tenant_id', $1, false), set_config('app.tenant_isolation', $2, false)\",\n342\t              ['', 'false']\n343\t            );\n344\t          } finally {\n345\t            client.release();\n346\t          }\n347\t        }\n348\t      });\n349\t\n350\t      // 错误时兜底释放连接（防止客户端断开导致连接泄漏）\n351\t      app.addHook('onError', async (request: FastifyRequest) => {\n352\t        const client = (request as any).dbClient as PoolClient | undefined;\n353\t        if (client) {\n354\t          try {\n355\t            await client.query('ROLLBACK').catch(() => {});\n356\t            client.release();\n357\t          } catch {}\n358\t        }\n359\t      });\n360\t\n361\t      // 客户端断开时释放连接\n362\t      app.addHook('onTimeout', async (request: FastifyRequest) => {\n363\t        const client = (request as any).dbClient as PoolClient | undefined;\n364\t        if (client) {\n365\t          try {\n366\t            client.release();\n367\t          } catch {}\n368\t        }\n369\t      });\n370\t    }\n371\t\n372\t    console.log('[Routes] Four-layer tenant isolation enabled');\n373\t {"parentUuid":"50e0812c-92dc-4ffb-aa10-57977aa81a4c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"a94df24a264e8dc9c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_12a177199699476dbd566d61","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Cross-Domain Orchestration API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * Prefix: /api/v1/orchestration\n5\t *\n6\t * Provides endpoints for creating, executing, pausing, resuming,\n7\t * and aborting cross-domain orchestration flows.\n8\t */\n9\t\n10\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n11\timport { DatabasePool } from '../services/database';\n12\timport { CrossDomainOrchestrator } from '../services/cross-domain-orchestration/CrossDomainOrchestrator';\n13\timport { DomainConnector } from '../services/cross-domain-orchestration/DomainConnector';\n14\timport { CrossDomainOrchestrationController } from './controllers/CrossDomainOrchestrationController';\n15\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n16\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n17\t\n18\texport interface CrossDomainRoutesOptions {\n19\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n20\t}\n21\t\n22\texport default async function crossDomainRoutes(\n23\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n24\t  options: CrossDomainRoutesOptions\n25\t): Promise<void> {\n26\t  // Initialize services\n27\t  const domainConnector = new DomainConnector();\n28\t  const orchestrator = new CrossDomainOrchestrator({\n29\t    domainConnector,\n30\t  });\n31\t  const controller = new CrossDomainOrchestrationController(orchestrator);\n32\t\n33\t  // POST /api/v1/orchestration - Create orchestration\n34\t  app.post('/v1/orchestration', { onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'cross_domain', action: 'write' })] }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n35\t    return controller.create(request, reply);\n36\t  });\n37\t\n38\t  // GET /api/v1/orchestration - List orchestrations\n39\t  app.get('/v1/orchestration', { onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'cross_domain', action: 'read' })] }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n40\t    return controller.list(request, reply);\n41\t  });\n42\t\n43\t  // GET /api/v1/orchestration/:id - Get orchestration details\n44\t  app.get('/v1/orchestration/:id', { onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'cross_domain', action: 'read' })] }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n45\t    return controller.getById(request, reply);\n46\t  });\n47\t\n48\t  // POST /api/v1/orchestration/:id/execute - Execute orchestration\n49\t  app.post('/v1/orchestration/:id/execute', { onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'cross_domain', action: 'execute' })] }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n50\t    return controller.execute(request, reply);\n51\t  });\n52\t\n53\t  // POST /api/v1/orchestration/:id/pause - Pause orchestration\n54\t  app.post('/v1/orchestration/:id/pause', { onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'cross_domain', action: 'execute' })] }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n55\t    return controller.pause(request, reply);\n56\t  });\n57\t\n58\t  // POST /api/v1/orchestration/:id/resume - Resume orchestration\n59\t  app.post('/v1/orchestration/:id/resume', { onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'cross_domain', action: 'execute' })] }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n60\t    return controller.resume(request, reply);\n61\t  });\n62\t\n63\t  // POST /api/v1/orchestration/:id/abort - Abort orchestration\n64\t  app.post('/v1/orchestration/:id/abort', { onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'cross_domain', action: 'execute' })] }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n65\t    return controller.abort(request, reply);\n66\t  });\n67\t}\n68\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"42e3dc29-5299-456e-b19c-f5165147963a","timestamp":"2026-05-22T08:05:23.232Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"50e0812c-92dc-4ffb-aa10-57977aa81a4c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}
{"parentUuid":"2418a1f5-24c9-446c-bfa1-95d3aa5cd4c6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"a94df24a264e8dc9c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_47796ba5c73d4b90ab275e85","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * LLM Trace API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * Routes under /api/v1/llm\n5\t */\n6\t\n7\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n8\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n9\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n10\timport { LLMTraceService, LLMTrace } from '../services/llm-trace/LLMTraceService';\n11\timport { CostCalculator } from '../services/llm-trace/CostCalculator';\n12\timport { LLMTraceRepository } from '../repositories/LLMTraceRepository';\n13\t\n14\tlet traceService: LLMTraceService | null = null;\n15\tlet costCalculator: CostCalculator | null = null;\n16\t\n17\texport function initLLMTrace(database?: { query: (text: string, params?: unknown[]) => Promise<{ rows: any[]; rowCount: number }> }): void {\n18\t  const repo = database ? new LLMTraceRepository(database) : undefined;\n19\t  traceService = new LLMTraceService(repo);\n20\t  costCalculator = new CostCalculator();\n21\t}\n22\t\n23\t// Default initialization (in-memory only, for backward compatibility)\n24\tinitLLMTrace();\n25\t\n26\tinterface TraceIdParams {\n27\t  traceId: string;\n28\t}\n29\t\n30\tinterface TraceQuery {\n31\t  tenantId?: number;\n32\t  scenarioId?: string;\n33\t  limit?: number;\n34\t}\n35\t\n36\tinterface DailyStatsQuery {\n37\t  tenantId: number;\n38\t  date?: string;\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\tinterface CostBreakdownQuery {\n42\t  tenantId: number;\n43\t  startDate?: string;\n44\t  endDate?: string;\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\texport async function llmTraceRoutes(app: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {\n48\t  const svc = traceService!;\n49\t  const calc = costCalculator!;\n50\t\n51\t  // GET /api/v1/llm/traces/:traceId - Get trace by ID\n52\t  app.get<{ Params: TraceIdParams }>(\n53\t    '/traces/:traceId',\n54\t    {\n55\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'llm_trace', action: 'read', extractResourceId: (req) => (req.params as { traceId: string }).traceId })],\n56\t    },\n57\t    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Params: TraceIdParams }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n58\t      const { traceId } = request.params;\n59\t      const trace = svc.getTrace(traceId);\n60\t      if (!trace) {\n61\t        return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'Trace not found', traceId });\n62\t      }\n63\t      return reply.send(trace);\n64\t    }\n65\t  );\n66\t\n67\t  // GET /api/v1/llm/traces - List traces with filters\n68\t  app.get<{ Querystring: TraceQuery }>(\n69\t    '/traces',\n70\t    {\n71\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'llm_trace', action: 'read' })],\n72\t    },\n73\t    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Querystring: TraceQuery }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n74\t      const { tenantId, scenarioId, limit = 100 } = request.query;\n75\t      let traces: LLMTrace[];\n76\t      if (tenantId) {\n77\t        traces = svc.getTracesByTenant(tenantId);\n78\t      } else if (scenarioId) {\n79\t        traces = svc.getTracesByScenario(scenarioId);\n80\t      } else {\n81\t        traces = [];\n82\t      }\n83\t      return reply.send({ data: traces.slice(0, limit), total: traces.length, limit });\n84\t    }\n85\t  );\n86\t\n87\t  // GET /api/v1/llm/stats/daily - Get daily aggregated statistics\n88\t  app.get<{ Querystring: DailyStatsQuery }>(\n89\t    '/stats/daily',\n90\t    {\n91\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'llm_trace', action: 'read' })],\n92\t    },\n93\t    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Querystring: DailyStatsQuery }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n94\t      const { tenantId, date } = request.query;\n95\t      const targetDate = date ? new Date(date) : new Date();\n96\t      const stats = await svc.aggregateDailyStats(tenantId, targetDate);\n97\t      return reply.send({ tenantId, date: targetDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10), ...stats });\n98\t    }\n99\t  );\n100\t\n101\t  // GET /api/v1/llm/cost/breakdown - Get cost breakdown\n102\t  app.get<{ Querystring: CostBreakdownQuery }>(\n103\t    '/cost/breakdown',\n104\t    {\n105\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'llm_trace', action: 'read' })],\n106\t    },\n107\t    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Querystring: CostBreakdownQuery }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n108\t      const { tenantId, startDate, endDate } = request.query;\n109\t      const traces = svc.getTracesByTenant(tenantId);\n110\t      let filteredTraces = traces;\n111\t      if (startDate) {\n112\t        const start = new Date(startDate);\n113\t        filteredTraces = filteredTraces.filter(t => t.requestStartedAt >= start);\n114\t      }\n115\t      if (endDate) {\n116\t        const end = new Date(endDate);\n117\t        filteredTraces = filteredTraces.filter(t => t.requestStartedAt <= end);\n118\t      }\n119\t      const breakdown = calc.calculateBatch(\n120\t        filteredTraces.map(t => ({ modelId: t.modelId, inputTokens: t.inputTokens, outputTokens: t.outputTokens }))\n121\t      );\n122\t      return reply.send({ tenantId, startDate, endDate, totalTraces: filteredTraces.length, ...breakdown });\n123\t    }\n124\t  );\n125\t\n126\t  // GET /api/v1/llm/tracking/accuracy - Get tracking accuracy metrics\n127\t  app.get(\n128\t    '/tracking/accuracy',\n129\t    {\n130\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'llm_trace', action: 'read' })],\n131\t    },\n132\t    async (_request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n133\t      const accuracy = svc.getTrackingAccuracy();\n134\t      const completed = svc.getCompletedCount();\n135\t      const failed = svc.getFailedCount();\n136\t      return reply.send({ accuracy, completed, failed, total: completed + failed, targetAccuracy: 0.98, meetsTarget: accuracy >= 0.98 });\n137\t    }\n138\t  );\n139\t\n140\t  // GET /api/v1/llm/pricing - Get model pricing table\n141\t  app.get(\n142\t    '/pricing',\n143\t    {\n144\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'llm_trace', action: 'read' })],\n145\t    },\n146\t    async (_request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n147\t      const pricing = calc.getAllPricing();\n148\t      return reply.send({ currency: 'CNY', unit: 'per token', pricing });\n149\t    }\n150\t  );\n151\t\n152\t  // POST /api/v1/llm/cost/estimate - Estimate cost for tokens\n153\t  app.post<{ Body: { modelId: string; inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number } }>(\n154\t    '/cost/estimate',\n155\t    {\n156\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'llm_trace', action: 'read' })],\n157\t    },\n158\t    async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Body: { modelId: string; inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number } }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n159\t      const { modelId, inputTokens, outputTokens } = request.body;\n160\t      if (!modelId || inputTokens === undefined || outputTokens === undefined) {\n161\t        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Missing required fields: modelId, inputTokens, outputTokens' });\n162\t      }\n163\t      const breakdown = calc.calculate(modelId, inputTokens, outputTokens);\n164\t      return reply.send({ modelId, inputTokens, outputTokens, ...breakdown });\n165\t    }\n166\t  );\n167\t}\n168\t\n169\texport default llmTraceRoutes;\n170\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"63b84470-f0af-4a80-8bd5-c3f2f4684705","timestamp":"2026-05-22T08:05:23.232Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2418a1f5-24c9-446c-bfa1-95d3aa5cd4c6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}
{"parentUuid":"4172c15d-3278-4a66-b852-5d91e9a8ac2b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"a94df24a264e8dc9c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_0e467215570e4df2b917fd58","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Hook Chain API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * 提供 Hook 链编排和管理的 API\n5\t * Prefix: /api/v1/hook-chains\n6\t */\n7\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n8\timport { HookChainService, HookChainDefinition } from '../services/hook-chain';\n9\timport { EventEmitter } from 'events';\n10\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n11\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n12\t\n13\tconst eventBus = new EventEmitter();\n14\tconst hookChainService = new HookChainService({ eventBus });\n15\t\n16\t/**\n17\t * 注册 Hook Chain API 路由\n18\t */\n19\texport default async function registerHookChainRoutes(app: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {\n20\t  // 事件广播到 Fastify\n21\t  hookChainService.on('chain:created', (event) => app.emit('hookchain:created', event));\n22\t  hookChainService.on('chain:started', (event) => app.emit('hookchain:started', event));\n23\t  hookChainService.on('chain:completed', (event) => app.emit('hookchain:completed', event));\n24\t  hookChainService.on('chain:failed', (event) => app.emit('hookchain:failed', event));\n25\t\n26\t  // ==================== Chain Management ====================\n27\t\n28\t  // POST /api/v1/hook-chains - 创建 Hook 链\n29\t  app.post('/hook-chains', {\n30\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'write' })],\n31\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Body: HookChainDefinition }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n32\t    try {\n33\t      const definition = request.body;\n34\t      const created = hookChainService.createChain(definition);\n35\t      return reply.status(201).send(created);\n36\t    } catch (error) {\n37\t      return reply.status(400).send({\n38\t        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Invalid chain definition',\n39\t      });\n40\t    }\n41\t  });\n42\t\n43\t  // GET /api/v1/hook-chains - 列出所有 Hook 链\n44\t  app.get('/hook-chains', {\n45\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'read' })],\n46\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n47\t    const chains = hookChainService.listChains();\n48\t    return reply.send({\n49\t      data: chains,\n50\t      total: chains.length,\n51\t    });\n52\t  });\n53\t\n54\t  // GET /api/v1/hook-chains/:chainId - 获取 Hook 链详情\n55\t  app.get('/hook-chains/:chainId', {\n56\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'read' })],\n57\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Params: { chainId: string } }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n58\t    const { chainId } = request.params;\n59\t    const chain = hookChainService.getChain(chainId);\n60\t\n61\t    if (!chain) {\n62\t      return reply.status(404).send({ error: `Hook chain \"${chainId}\" not found` });\n63\t    }\n64\t\n65\t    return reply.send(chain);\n66\t  });\n67\t\n68\t  // PUT /api/v1/hook-chains/:chainId - 更新 Hook 链\n69\t  app.put('/hook-chains/:chainId', {\n70\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'write' })],\n71\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Params: { chainId: string }; Body: Partial<HookChainDefinition> }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n72\t    const { chainId } = request.params;\n73\t    const updates = request.body;\n74\t\n75\t    const updated = hookChainService.updateChain(chainId, updates);\n76\t\n77\t    if (!updated) {\n78\t      return reply.status(404).send({ error: `Hook chain \"${chainId}\" not found` });\n79\t    }\n80\t\n81\t    return reply.send(updated);\n82\t  });\n83\t\n84\t  // DELETE /api/v1/hook-chains/:chainId - 删除 Hook 链\n85\t  app.delete('/hook-chains/:chainId', {\n86\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'write' })],\n87\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Params: { chainId: string } }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n88\t    const { chainId } = request.params;\n89\t    const deleted = hookChainService.deleteChain(chainId);\n90\t\n91\t    if (!deleted) {\n92\t      return reply.status(404).send({ error: `Hook chain \"${chainId}\" not found` });\n93\t    }\n94\t\n95\t    return reply.status(204).send();\n96\t  });\n97\t\n98\t  // ==================== Chain Execution ====================\n99\t\n100\t  // POST /api/v1/hook-chains/:chainId/execute - 执行 Hook 链\n101\t  app.post('/hook-chains/:chainId/execute', {\n102\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'write' })],\n103\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest<{\n104\t    Params: { chainId: string };\n105\t    Body: {\n106\t      triggerSource: string;\n107\t      triggerPayload: Record<string, any>;\n108\t      tenantId: string;\n109\t    };\n110\t  }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n111\t    const { chainId } = request.params;\n112\t    const { triggerSource, triggerPayload, tenantId } = request.body;\n113\t\n114\t    try {\n115\t      const result = await hookChainService.executeChain(chainId, triggerSource, triggerPayload, tenantId);\n116\t      return reply.send(result);\n117\t    } catch (error) {\n118\t      return reply.status(500).send({\n119\t        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Chain execution failed',\n120\t        chainId,\n121\t      });\n122\t    }\n123\t  });\n124\t\n125\t  // GET /api/v1/hook-chains/:chainId/history - 获取执行历史\n126\t  app.get('/hook-chains/:chainId/history', {\n127\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'read' })],\n128\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Params: { chainId: string } }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n129\t    const { chainId } = request.params;\n130\t    const history = hookChainService.getExecutionHistory(chainId);\n131\t\n132\t    return reply.send({\n133\t      chainId,\n134\t      history,\n135\t      total: history.length,\n136\t    });\n137\t  });\n138\t\n139\t  // GET /api/v1/hook-chains/executions/pending - 获取正在执行的链\n140\t  app.get('/hook-chains/executions/pending', {\n141\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'read' })],\n142\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n143\t    const pending = hookChainService.getPendingExecutions();\n144\t    return reply.send({\n145\t      pending,\n146\t      total: pending.length,\n147\t    });\n148\t  });\n149\t\n150\t  // ==================== SSE Events ====================\n151\t\n152\t  // GET /api/v1/hook-chains/events - SSE 实时事件推送\n153\t  app.get('/hook-chains/events', {\n154\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'read' })],\n155\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n156\t    reply.raw.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/event-stream');\n157\t    reply.raw.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache');\n158\t    reply.raw.setHeader('Connection', 'keep-alive');\n159\t\n160\t    reply.raw.write('event: connected\\ndata: {\"message\":\"Hook chain events connected\"}\\n\\n');\n161\t\n162\t    const eventHandler = (event: any) => {\n163\t      reply.raw.write(`event: ${event.type || 'update'}\\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(event)}\\n\\n`);\n164\t    };\n165\t\n166\t    app.on('hookchain:created', eventHandler);\n167\t    app.on('hookchain:started', eventHandler);\n168\t    app.on('hookchain:completed', eventHandler);\n169\t    app.on('hookchain:failed', eventHandler);\n170\t\n171\t    request.raw.on('close', () => {\n172\t      app.removeListener('hookchain:created', eventHandler);\n173\t      app.removeListener('hookchain:started', eventHandler);\n174\t      app.removeListener('hookchain:completed', eventHandler);\n175\t      app.removeListener('hookchain:failed', eventHandler);\n176\t    });\n177\t\n178\t    return reply;\n179\t  });\n180\t\n181\t  // ==================== Custom Executor ====================\n182\t\n183\t  // POST /api/v1/hook-chains/executors - 注册自定义执行器\n184\t  app.post('/hook-chains/executors', {\n185\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'hook', action: 'write' })],\n186\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Body: { type: string; handler: string } }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n187\t    const { type, handler } = request.body;\n188\t\n189\t    // 注意：这里只是示例，实际需要安全的执行器注册机制\n190\t    reply.status(501).send({\n191\t      error: 'Custom executor registration requires secure implementation',\n192\t      type,\n193\t    });\n194\t  });\n195\t}\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"928f865e-0aa6-4f8b-a6d4-a2abcca61e3f","timestamp":"2026-05-22T08:05:23.233Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4172c15d-3278-4a66-b852-5d91e9a8ac2b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}
{"parentUuid":"c2cd7799-ed93-4be3-856f-1a8b7a8135f9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"a94df24a264e8dc9c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_9ecdbe64698b47019dab10f0","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Skill Management API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * Routes under /api/v1/skills\n5\t * Migrated to PostgreSQL Repository pattern (M12)\n6\t *\n7\t * Includes:\n8\t * - Skill CRUD\n9\t * - Version SkillPermissions = {\n29\t  READ:    { resource: 'skill', action: 'read' },\n30\t  WRITE:   { resource: 'skill', action: 'write' },\n31\t  USE:     { resource: 'skill', action: 'use' },\n32\t  INSTALL: { resource: 'skill', action: 'install' },\n33\t  CONFIG:  { resource: 'skill', action: 'config' },\n34\t  ADMIN:   { resource: 'skill', action: 'admin' },\n35\t};\n36\t\n37\tinterface SkillRoutesOptions {\n38\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\texport default async function skillRoutes(\n42\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n43\t  options: SkillRoutesOptions\n44\t): Promise<void> {\n45\t  // Initialize Repository and Service with database pool\n46\t  const repository = options.database\n47\t    ? new SkillRepository(options.database)\n48\t    : undefined;\n49\t\n50\t  if (!repository) {\n51\t    console.warn('[SkillRoutes] No database pool provided, skill routes will not be functional');\n52\t    return;\n53\t  }\n54\t\n55\t  const service = new SkillService(repository);\n56\t  const controller = new SkillController(service);\n57\t\n58\t  // ==================== Skill CRUD ====================\n59\t\n60\t  // GET /api/v1/skills — list/search skills\n61\t  app.get('/', {\n62\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.READ)],\n63\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n64\t    return controller.list(request, reply);\n65\t  });\n66\t\n67\t  // GET /api/v1/skills/:id — skill detail\n68\t  app.get('/:id', {\n69\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ ...SkillPermissions.READ, extractResourceId: (req) => (req.params as { id: string }).id })],\n70\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n71\t    return controller.getDetail(request, reply);\n72\t  });\n73\t\n74\t  // POST /api/v1/skills — create skill\n75\t  app.post('/', {\n76\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.WRITE)],\n77\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n78\t    return controller.create(request, reply);\n79\t  });\n80\t\n81\t  // PUT }).id })],\n84\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n85\t    return controller.update(request, reply);\n86\t  });\n87\t\n88\t  // DELETE /api/v1/skills/:id — delete controller.delete(request, reply);\n93\t  });\n94\t\n95\t  // ==================== Version Management ====================\n96\t\n97\t  // GET /api/v1/skills/:id/versions — list versions\n98\t  app.get('/:id/versions', {\n99\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ ...SkillPermissions.READ, extractResourceId: (req) => (req.params as { id: string }).id })],\n100\t  }, async app.post('/:id/versions', {\n106\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ ...SkillPermissions.WRITE, extractResourceId: (req) => (req.params as { id: string }).id })],\n107\t  }, async ====================\n112\t\n113\t  // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/install — increment install count\n114\t  app.post('/:id/install', {\n115\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.INSTALL)],\n116\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n117\t    return controller.install(request, reply);\n118\t  });\n119\t\n120\t  // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/uninstall — decrement install count\n121\t  app.post('/:id/uninstall', {\n122\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.INSTALL)],\n123\t  }, async // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/rate — add rating\n130\t  app.post('/:id/rate', {\n131\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ ...SkillPermissions.WRITE, extractResourceId: (req) => (req.params as { id: string }).id })],\n132\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n133\t    return controller.rate(request, reply);\n134\t  });\n135\t\n136\t  // {\n142\t    return controller.listInstances(request, reply);\n143\t  });\n144\t\n145\t  // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/instances — create instance\n146\t  app.post('/:id/instances', {\n147\t    onRequest: });\n151\t\n152\t  // PUT /api/v1/skills/:id/instances/:instanceId — update instance\n153\t  app.put('/:id/instances/:instanceId', {\n154\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.CONFIG)],\n155\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n156\t    return controller.updateInstance(request, reply);\n157\t  });\n158\t\n159\t  // DELETE /api/v1/skills/:id/instances/:instanceId — delete instance\n160\t  app.delete('/:id/instances/:instanceId', {\n161\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.CONFIG)],\n162\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n163\t    return controller.deleteInstance(request, reply);\n164\t  });\n165\t\n166\t  // ==================== Direct Execution ====================\n167\t\n168\t  // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/execute — direct execution (non-Pipeline)\n169\t  app.post('/:id/execute', {\n170\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.USE)],\n171\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n172\t    return controller.executeSkill(request, reply);\n173\t  });\n174\t\n175\t  // GET /api/v1/skills/:id/executions — execution history for a skill\n176\t  app.get('/:id/executions', {\n177\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ ...SkillPermissions.READ, extractResourceId: (req) => (req.params as { id: string }).id })],\n178\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n179\t    return controller.listExecutions(request, reply);\n180\t  });\n181\t\n182\t  // GET /api/v1/skills/executions — all executions (admin)\n183\t  app.get('/executions', {\n184\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.ADMIN)],\n185\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n186\t    return controller.listAllExecutions(request, reply);\n187\t  });\n188\t\n189\t  // ==================== Review Workflow ====================\n190\t\n191\t  // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/submit — submit for review\n192\t  app.post('/:id/submit', {\n193\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ ...SkillPermissions.WRITE, extractResourceId: (req) => (req.params as { id: string }).id })],\n194\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n195\t    return controller.submitForReview(request, reply);\n196\t  });\n197\t\n198\t  // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/approve — approve skill\n199\t  app.post('/:id/approve', {\n200\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.ADMIN)],\n201\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n202\t    return controller.approveSkill(request, reply);\n203\t  });\n204\t\n205\t  // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/reject — reject skill\n206\t  app.post('/:id/reject', {\n207\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.ADMIN)],\n208\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n209\t    return controller.rejectSkill(request, reply);\n210\t  });\n211\t\n212\t  // POST /api/v1/skills/:id/archive — archive/unpublish skill\n213\t  app.post('/:id/archive', {\n214\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission(SkillPermissions.ADMIN)],\n215\t  }, async {"parentUuid":"30b42f95-27a1-4090-afd6-533d1067311d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"a94df24a264e8dc9c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_7063f58270e14cbebf47598a","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * AI Agent API 路由注册\n3\t *\n4\t * 提供 Agent 管理、执行、审计日志等 API\n5\t */\n6\t\n7\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest } from 'fastify';\n8\timport { BaseAgent } from '../services/ai-agents/base/BaseAgent';\n9\timport pino from 'pino';\n10\t\n11\tconst logger = pino({ name: 'ai-agent-routes' });\n12\t\n13\t/**\n14\t * Agent 注册表（全局单例）\n15\t * 实际生产中应由 AgentRegistry 管理\n16\t */\n17\tconst agentRegistry = new Map<string, BaseAgent>();\n18\t\n19\t/**\n20\t * 注册 Agent 实例\n21\t */\n22\texport function registerAgent(id: string, agent: BaseAgent): void {\n23\t  agentRegistry.set(id, agent);\n24\t  logger.info({ agentId: id }, 'Agent registered');\n25\t}\n26\t\n27\t/**\n28\t * 注册所有 AI Agent 路由\n29\t */\n30\texport function registerAIAgentRoutes(app: FastifyInstance): void {\n31\t  const prefix = '/api/v1/ai-agents';\n32\t\n33\t  // 获取所有 Agent 列表\n34\t  app.get(`${prefix}/list`, async (request, reply) => {\n35\t    const agents = Array.from(agentRegistry.entries()).map(([id, agent]) => ({\n36\t      id,\n37\t      config: agent.getConfig(),\n38\t      status: (agent as any).status,\n39\t    }));\n40\t\n41\t    return { success: true, data: agents };\n42\t  });\n43\t\n44\t  // 获取单个 Agent 详情\n45\t  app.get(`${prefix}/:id`, async (request, reply) => {\n46\t    const { id } = request.params as { id: string };\n47\t    const agent = agentRegistry.get(id);\n48\t    if (!agent) {\n49\t      return reply.code(404).send({ success: false, error: 'Agent not found' });\n50\t    }\n51\t\n52\t    return {\n53\t      success: true,\n54\t      data: {\n55\t        id,\n56\t        config: agent.getConfig(),\n57\t        status: (agent as any).status,\n58\t      },\n59\t    };\n60\t  });\n61\t\n62\t  // 获取 Agent 审计日志\n63\t  app.get(`${prefix}/:id/audit-logs`, async (request, reply) => {\n64\t    const { id } = request.params as { id: string };\n65\t    const agent = agentRegistry.get(id);\n66\t    if (!agent) {\n67\t      return reply.code(404).send({ success: false, error: 'Agent not found' });\n68\t    }\n69\t\n70\t    const limit = parseInt((request.query as any)?.limit || '100', 10);\n71\t    const logs = agent.getAuditLog(limit);\n72\t\n73\t    return { success: true, data: logs };\n74\t  });\n75\t\n76\t  // 执行 Agent\n77\t  app.post(`${prefix}/:id/execute`, async (request, reply) => {\n78\t    const { id } = request.params as { id: string };\n79\t    const agent = agentRegistry.get(id);\n80\t    if (!agent) {\n81\t      return reply.code(404).send({ success: false, error: 'Agent not found' });\n82\t    }\n83\t\n84\t    const input = request.body as Record<string, any>;\n85\t    try {\n86\t      const result = await agent.execute(input);\n87\t      return { success: true, data: result };\n88\t    } catch (error) {\n89\t      logger.error({ agentId: id, error }, 'Agent execution failed');\n90\t      return reply.code(500).send({\n91\t        success: false,\n92\t        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error',\n93\t      });\n94\t    }\n95\t  });\n96\t\n97\t  logger.info('AI Agent routes registered');\n98\t}\n99\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. '../services/chatops/SSEConnectionManager';\n38\timport { InputValidator } from '../services/chatops/InputValidator';\n39\timport { DeployService } from '../services/deploy/DeployService';\n40\timport '../services/chatops/RateLimitService';\n51\timport { WebhookService } from '../services/chatops/WebhookService';\n52\t\n53\tinterface ChatOpsRoutesOptions {\n54\t  eventBus?: EventBusService;\n55\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n56\t  /** 可选注入的外部服务，用于 CommandRouter 真实 handler */\n57\t  pipelineService?: any;\n58\t  deployService?: any;\n59\t  monitoringService?: any;\n60\t  diagnosticService?: any;\n61\t  selfHealingService?: any;\n62\t}\n63\t\n64\texport default async function chatopsRoutes(\n65\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n66\t  options: ChatOpsRoutesOptions,\n67\t): Promise<void> {\n68\t  if (!options.database) {\n69\t    console.warn('[ChatOpsRoutes] No database pool provided, chatops routes will not be functional');\n70\t    return;\n71\t  }\n72\t\n73\t  const db = options.database;\n74\t\n75\t  // ==================== Existing Repositories ====================\n76\t  const commandRepo = new ChatOpsCommandRepository(db);\n77\t  const executionRepo = new ChatOpsExecutionRepository(db);\n78\t  const sessionRepo = new ChatOpsSessionRepository(db);\n79\t  const auditRepo = new ChatOpsAuditLogRepository(db);\n80\t\n81\t  // ==================== Phase 1a New Repositories ====================\n82\t  const messageRepo = new ChatOpsMessageRepository(db);\n83\t  const notifPrefRepo = new ChatOpsNotificationPreferenceRepository(db);\n84\t  const dndRepo = new ChatOpsDNDSettingsRepository(db);\n85\t  const alertStateRepo = new ChatOpsAlertStateRepository(db);\n86\t\n87\t  // ==================== Existing Services ====================\n88\t  const commandService = new CommandService({\n89\t    pool: db,\n90\t    eventBus: options.eventBus,\n91\t    repository: commandRepo,\n92\t  });\n93\t\n94\t  // InputValidator: 安全校验服务，注册所有命令 schema\n95\t  const inputValidator = new InputValidator();\n96\t\n97\t  // CommandRouter: 注册真实服务 handler (若注入)，否则使用内置 mock handler\n98\t  const serviceMap = new Map<string, any>();\n99\t  const commandRouter = new CommandRouter(serviceMap);\n100\t\n101\t  // 注册真实服务 handler\n102\t  if (options.pipelineService) {\n103\t    commandRouter.registerHandler('pipeline', async (params: Record<string, unknown>) => {\n104\t      const pipelineId = params.pipelineId as string;\n105\t      if (!pipelineId) throw new Error('pipelineId 必填');\n106\t      try {\n107\t        const pipeline = await options.pipelineService.getById(pipelineId);\n108\t        return {\n109\t          status: 'ok',\n110\t          command: 'pipeline',\n111\t          pipeline,\n112\t          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n113\t        };\n114\t      } catch (err) {\n115\t        return {\n116\t          status: 'error',\n117\t          command: 'pipeline',\n118\t          error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Pipeline lookup failed',\n119\t          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n120\t        };\n121\t      }\n122\t    });\n123\t    commandRouter.registerHandler('status', async (params: Record<string, unknown>) => {\n124\t      const pipelineId = params.pipelineId as string;\n125\t      if (pipelineId) {\n126\t        try {\n127\t          const pipeline = await options.pipelineService.getById(pipelineId);\n128\t          return {\n129\t            status: 'ok',\n130\t            command: 'status',\n131\t            resource: 'pipeline',\n132\t            data: pipeline,\n133\t            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n134\t          };\n135\t        } catch (err) {\n136\t          return {\n137\t            status: 'error',\n138\t            command: 'status',\n139\t            error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Status lookup failed',\n140\t            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n141\t          };\n142\t        }\n143\t      }\n144\t      return {\n145\t        status: 'ok',\n146\t        command: 'status',\n147\t        system: 'healthy',\n148\t        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n149\t      };\n150\t    });\n151\t  }\n152\t\n153\t  // 本地初始化服务（若未注入则使用注入的服务）\n154\t  const deployService = options.deployService || (new DeployService(new DeployRepository(db)));\n155\t  const monitoringService string) || 'default';\n166\t    if (!service || !environment) {\n167\t      return { status: 'error', command: 'deploy', error: 'service 和 environment 必填', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };\n168\t    }\n169\t    try {\n170\t      const deployment = await deployService.createDeployment({\n171\t        tenant_id: tenantId,\n172\t        environment,\n173\t        config: { service, Date().toISOString() };\n177\t    } catch (err) {\n178\t      return { status: 'error', command: 'deploy', error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Deploy failed', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };\n179\t    }\n180\t  });\n181\t\n182\t  commandRouter.registerHandler('rollback', async (params: Record<string, unknown>) => {\n183\t    const deployment = params.deployment as deployment, targetVersion: targetVersion || 'previous', previousVersion: dep.config?.version || 'unknown', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };\n191\t    } catch (err) {\n192\t      return { status: commandRouter.registerHandler('restart', async (params: Record<string, unknown>) => {\n197\t    const namespace = params.namespace as string;\n198\t    const pod = params.pod as string;\n199\t    if function getSkill(id: string) {\n69\t  return api.get(`/v1/skills/${id}`);\n70\t}\n71\t\n72\texport function createSkill(data: SkillPackageInput) {\n73\t  return api.post('/v1/skills', data);\n74\t}\n75\t\n76\texport function updateSkill(id: string, data: UpdateSkillInput) {\n77\t  return api.put(`/v1/skills/${id}`, data);\n78\t}\n79\t\n80\texport function deleteSkill(id: string) {\n81\t  return api.delete(`/v1/skills/${id}`);\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\t// ---- Skill Versions ----\n85\t\n86\texport function getSkillVersions(id: string) {\n87\t  return api.post(`/v1/skills/${id}/install`);\n94\t}\n95\t\n96\texport function uninstallSkill(id: string) {\n97\t  return api.post(`/v1/skills/${id}/uninstall`);\n98\t}\n99\t\n100\t// ---- Rating ----\n101\t\n102\texport function rateSkill(id: string, data: SkillRating) {\n103\t  return api.post(`/v1/skills/${id}/rate`, data);\n104\t}\n105\t\n106\t// ---- My Skills ----\n107\t// Note: Backend skill-routes.ts doesn't have /my endpoints.\n108\t// Use query params on /skills to filter by installed status.\n109\t\n110\texport function getMySkills() {\n111\t  return api.get('/v1/skills', { params: { installed: 'true' } });\n112\t}\n113\t\n114\texport function getInstalledSkill(id: string) {\n115\t  return api.get(`/v1/skills/${id}`);\n116\t}\n117\t\n118\t// ---- Instance Management ----\n119\t\n120\texport interface SkillInstance {\n121\t  id: string;\n122\t  skillId: string;\n123\t  tenantId: string;\n124\t  projectId?: string;\n125\t  name: string;\n126\t  description?: SkillInstance[]) || [] } };\n164\t}\n165\t\n166\texport async function createSkillInstance(skillId: string, data: CreateInstanceInput) {\n167\t  const res = await api.post(`/v1/skills/${skillId}/instances`, data);\n168\t  const body = res.data as any;\n169\t  return { data: { data: body?.data as SkillInstance } };\n170\t}\n171\t\n172\texport async function res.data as any;\n175\t  return { data: { data: body?.data as SkillInstance } };\n176\t}\n177\t\n178\texport async function deleteSkillInstance(skillId: string, instanceId: string) {\n179\t  const res number;\n211\t}\n212\t\n213\texport async function executeSkill(skillId: string, data: ExecuteSkillInput) {\n214\t  const res = await api.post(`/v1/skills/${skillId}/execute`, data);\n215\t  const limit?: number }) {\n220\t  const res = await api.get(`/v1/skills/${skillId}/executions`, { params });\n221\t  const body = res.data as any;\n222\t  const rawExecutions = body?.data?.executions || [];\n223\t  // Map snake_case backend fields to camelCase frontend types\n224\t  const executions: SkillExecution[] = rawExecutions.map((e: any) => ({\n225\t    id: e.id,\n226\t    skillId: e.skill_id,\n227\t    instanceId: e.instance_id,\n228\t    tenantId: e.tenant_id,\n229\t    userId: e.triggered_by,\n230\t    capability: e.capability,\n231\t    input: e.input || {},\n232\t api.post(`/v1/chatops/alerts/${alertId}/dismiss`);\n275\t}\n276\t\n277\t// ---- SSE Stream ----\n278\t\n279\t/**\n280\t * ARCH-005: SSE 连接配置\n281\t * 与后端 EventBusService 配置对齐\n282\t */\n283\texport interface SSEConnectionConfig {\n284\t  /** 最大重连次数（与后端 NATS maxReconnectAttempts 对齐） */\n285\t  maxReconnectAttempts: number;\n286\t  /** 初始延迟（与后端 reconnectTimeWait 对齐） */\n287\t  initialDelayMs: SSEConnectionConfig = {\n296\t  maxReconnectAttempts: 20, // 与后端 NATS 默认对齐\n297\t  initialDelayMs: 2000, // 与后端 reconnectTimeWait 对齐\n298\t  maxDelayMs: 30000,\n299\t  healthCheckIntervalMs: 10000, // 每 10s 检查后端健康状态\n300\t};\n301\t\n302\texport interface SSEConnectionOptions {\n303\t  onMessage: (data: unknown) => void;\n304\t  onReconnect?: (attempt: number) => void;\n305\t  onError?: (error: Error) => void;\n306\t  /** ARCH-005: 可选的配置覆盖 */\n307\t  config?: Partial<SSEConnectionConfig>;\n308\t  /** ARCH-005: 后端健康状态变化回调 */\n309\t  onHealthChange?: (healthy: boolean, fallback: boolean) => void;\n310\t}\n311\t\n312\tinterface SSEConnectionState {\n313\t  eventSource: EventSource | null;\n314\t  reconnectTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null;\n315\t  healthCheckTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;\n316\t  attempt: number;\n317\t  disposed: boolean;\n318\t  /** ARCH-005: 后端健康状态 */\n319\t  backendHealthy: boolean;\n320\t  backendFallback: boolean;\n321\t}\n322\t\n323\tlet sseState: SSEConnectionState | null = null;\n324\t\n325\t/**\n326\t * 计算指数退避延迟 (1s, 2s, 4s, 8s, ... 最大 30s)\n327\t */\n328\tfunction getReconnectDelay(attempt: number): number {\n329\t  return Math.min(1000 * Math.pow(2, attempt), 30_000);\n330\t}\n331\t\n332\t/**\n333\t * ARCH-005: 检查后端健康状态\n334\t */\n335\tasync function checkBackendHealth(): Promise<{ healthy: boolean; fallback: boolean }> {\n336\t  try {\n337\t    const response = await fetch('/api/v1/chatops/health');\n338\t    if (!response.ok) {\n339\t      return { healthy: false, fallback: false };\n340\t    }\n341\t    const data = await response.json();\n342\t    const status = data?.eventBus?.status || 'down';\n343\t    return {\n344\t      healthy: status === 'up',\n345\t      fallback: status === 'fallback',\n346\t    };\n347\t  } catch {\n348\t    return { healthy: false, fallback: false };\n349\t  }\n350\t}\n351\t\n352\t/**\n353\t * 建立 SSE 连接，支持断线自动重连\n354\t * ARCH-005: 添加健康检查感知，智能重连策略\n355\t */\n356\texport function connectSSE(options: SSEConnectionOptions): void {\n357\t  // 如果已有连接，先断开\n358\t  disconnectSSE();\n359\t\n360\t  const config = { ...DEFAULT_SSE_CONFIG, ...options.config };\n361\t\n362\t  sseState = {\n363\t    eventSource: null,\n364\t    reconnectTimer: null,\n365\t    healthCheckTimer: null,\n366\t    attempt: 0,\n367\t    disposed: false,\n368\t    backendHealthy: true,\n369\t    backendFallback: false,\n370\t  };\n371\t\n372\t  // ARCH-005: 启动健康检查定时器\n373\t  sseState.healthCheckTimer = setInterval(async () => {\n374\t    if (sseState?.disposed) return;\n375\t\n376\t    const health = await checkBackendHealth();\n377\t    const prevHealthy = sseState?.backendHealthy;\n378\t    const prevFallback = sseState?.backendFallback;\n379\t\n380\t    sseState!.backendHealthy = health.healthy;\n381\t    sseState!.backendFallback = health.fallback;\n382\t\n383\t    // ARCH-005: 健康状态变化时通知调用方\n384\t    if (prevHealthy !== health.healthy || prevFallback !== health.fallback) {\n385\t      options.onHealthChange?.(health.healthy, health.fallback);\n386\t    }\n387\t\n388\t    // ARCH-005: 后端恢复健康且有连接问题时立即重试\n389\t    if (health.healthy && !sseState?.eventSource && (sseState?.attempt ?? 0) > 0) {\n390\t      console.log('[SSE] Backend recovered, attempting immediate reconnect');\n391\t      sseState!.attempt = 0; // 重置重试计数\n392\t      doConnect();\n393\t    }\n394\t  }, config.healthCheckIntervalMs);\n395\t\n396\t  function doConnect(): void {\n397\t    if (sseState!.disposed) return;\n398\t\n399\t    // ARCH-005: 后端处于 fallback 模式时降低重连频率\n400\t    if (sseState!.backendFallback) {\n401\t      console.log('[SSE] Backend in fallback mode, reducing reconnect frequency');\n402\t    }\n403\t\n404\t    try {\n405\t      const es = new EventSource('/api/v1/chatops/stream/recommendations');\n406\t      sseState!.eventSource = es;\n407\t\n408\t      es.onopen = () => {\n409\t        // 连接成功，重置重试计数\n410\t        sseState!.attempt = 0;\n411\t        sseState!.backendHealthy = true;\n412\t        options.onHealthChange?.(true, false);\n413\t      };\n414\t\n415\t      es.addEventListener('recommendations', (event: MessageEvent) => {\n416\t        try {\n417\t          const data = JSON.parse(event.data);\n418\t          options.onMessage(data);\n419\t        } catch {\n420\t          // 忽略解析错误\n421\t        }\n422\t      });\n423\t\n424\t      // 监听未命名事件 (data-only messages)\n425\t      es.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {\n426\t        try {\n427\t          const data = JSON.parse(event.data);\n428\t          options.onMessage(data);\n429\t        } catch {\n430\t          // 忽略解析错误\n431\t        }\n432\t      };\n433\t\n434\t      // ARCH-005: 监听后端发送的 shutdown 事件\n435\t      es.addEventListener('shutdown', (event: MessageEvent) => {\n436\t        try {\n437\t          const data = JSON.parse(event.data);\n438\t          console.log('[SSE] Server shutdown:', data.reason);\n439\t          // 后端主动关闭时等待健康检查触发重连\n440\t        } catch {\n441\t          // 忽略解析错误\n442\t        }\n443\t      });\n444\t\n445\t      es.onerror = () => {\n446\t        es.close();\n447\t        sseState!.eventSource = null;\n448\t\n449\t        if (sseState!.disposed) return;\n450\t\n451\t        // 立即递增 attempt，确保退避延迟正确递增\n452\t        sseState!.attempt++;\n453\t\n454\t        // ARCH-005: 使用配置中的最大重连次数\n455\t        if (sseState!.attempt >= config.maxReconnectAttempts) {\n456\t          options.onError?.(new Error('SSE sseState.eventSource = null;\n495\t    }\n496\t\n497\t    if (sseState.reconnectTimer) {\n498\t      clearTimeout(sseState.reconnectTimer);\n499\t      sseState.reconnectTimer = null;\n500\t    }\n501\t\n502\t    // ARCH-005: 清理健康检查定时器\n503\t    if (sseState.healthCheckTimer) {\n504\t      clearInterval(sseState.healthCheckTimer);\n505\t      sseState.healthCheckTimer = null;\n506\t    }\n507\t\n508\t    sseState = null;\n509\t  }\n510\t}\n511\t\n512\t/**\n513\t * 获取当前 SSE 连接状态\n514\t * ARCH-005: 返回健康状态信息\n515\t */\n516\texport function getSSEState(): {\n517\t  connected: boolean;\n518\t  attempt: number;\n519\t  backendHealthy: boolean;\n520\t  backendFallback: boolean;\n521\t} {\n522\t  if (!sseState) {\n523\t    return { connected: false, attempt: 0, backendHealthy: Typography;\n25\t\n26\t/**\n27\t * AI Agent 管理主页面\n28\t * 包含 Agent 列表、详情、执行、审计日志\n29\t */\n30\tconst AIAgentsManagement: React.FC = () => {\n31\t  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('list');\n32\t  const [agents, setAgents] = useState<AgentInfo[]>([]);\n33\t  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n34\t  const [detailDrawerOpen, setDetailDrawerOpen] = [auditLogLoading, setAuditLogLoading] = useState(false);\n38\t  const [executeModalOpen, setExecuteModalOpen] = useState(false);\n39\t  const [executing, setExecuting] = useState(false);\n40\t  const [executionResult, setExecutionResult] = useState<AgentExecutionResult | null>(null);\n41\t  const [form] = Form.useForm();\n42\t\n43\t  // 加载 Agent 列表\n44\t  const loadAgents = async () => {\n45\t    setLoading(true);\n46\t    try {\n47\t      const response = await aiAgentApi.getList();\n48\t      const apiData = response.data as any;\n49\t      if (apiData.success && apiData.data) {\n50\t        setAgents(apiData.data);\n51\t      } else {\n52\t        setAgents([]);\n53\t      }\n54\t    } catch (error) {\n55\t      console.error('Failed to load agents:', error);\n56\t      message.error('加载 Agent 列表失败');\n57\t      setAgents([]);\n58\t    } finally {\n59\t      setLoading(false);\n60\t    }\n61\t  };\n62\t\n63\t  useEffect(() => {\n64\t    loadAgents();\n65\t  }, []);\n66\t\n67\t  // 查看详情\n68\t  const handleViewDetail = (agent: AgentInfo) => {\n69\t    setSelectedAgent(agent);\n70\t    setDetailDrawerOpen(true);\n71\t  };\n72\t\n73\t  // 查看审计日志\n74\t  const handleViewAuditLog = async (agent: AgentInfo) => {\n75\t    setSelectedAgent(agent);\n76\t    setAuditLogs([]);\n77\t    setAuditLogLoading(true);\n78\t    setActiveTab('audit');\n79\t    try {\n80\t      const response = await aiAgentApi.getAuditLogs(agent.id);\n81\t      const apiData = response.data as any;\n82\t      if (apiData.success && apiData.data) {\n83\t        setAuditLogs(apiData.data);\n84\t      } else {\n85\t        setAuditLogs([]);\n86\t      }\n87\t    } catch (error) {\n88\t      console.error('Failed to load audit logs:', error);\n89\t      message.error('加载审计日志失败');\n90\t      setAuditLogs([]);\n91\t    } finally {\n92\t      setAuditLogLoading(false);\n93\t    }\n94\t  };\n95\t\n96\t  // 执行 Agent\n97\t  const handleExecute = (agent: AgentInfo) => {\n98\t    setSelectedAgent(agent);\n99\t    setExecutionResult(null);\n100\t    form.resetFields();\n101\t    setExecuteModalOpen(true);\n102\t  };\n103\t\n104\t  // 提交执行\n105\t  const handleExecuteSubmit = async () => {\n106\t    if (!selectedAgent) return;\n107\t    setExecuting(true);\n108\t    try {\n109\t      const values = form.getFieldsValue();\n110\t      let input: Record<string, any> = {};\n111\t      try {\n112\t        // 尝试解析 JSON 输入\n113\t        if (values.input && typeof values.input === 'string') {\n114\t          input = JSON.parse(values.input);\n115\t        } else if (values.input && typeof values.input === 'object') {\n116\t          input = values.input;\n117\t        }\n118\t      } catch {\n119\t        message.error('请输入合法的 JSON 格式');\n120\t        setExecuting(false);\n121\t        return;\n122\t      }\n123\t\n124\t      const response = await aiAgentApi.execute(selectedAgent.id, input);\n125\t      const apiData = response.data as any;\n126\t      if (apiData.success) {\n127\t        setExecutionResult(apiData.data as AgentExecutionResult);\n128\t        message.success('Agent 执行成功');\n129\t      } else {\n130\t        setExecutionResult({\n131\t          success: false,\n132\t          error: apiData.error || '执行失败',\n133\t        });\n134\t        message.error('Agent 执行失败');\n135\t      }\n136\t    } catch (error) {\n137\t      console.error('Failed to execute agent:', error);\n138\t      setExecutionResult({\n139\t        success: false,\n140\t        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : key: 'list',\n151\t      label: (\n152\t        <span>\n153\t          <RobotOutlined />\n154\t          Agent 列表\n155\t        </span>\n156\t      ),\n157\t      children: (\n158\t        <AgentList\n159\t          agents={agents}\n160\t          loading={loading}\n161\t          onViewDetail={handleViewDetail}\n162\t          onExecute={handleExecute}\n163\t          onViewAuditLog={handleViewAuditLog}\n164\t        />\n165\t      ),\n166\t    },\n167\t    {\n168\t      key: 'audit',\n169\t      label: (\n170\t        <span>\n171\t          <FileTextOutlined />\n172\t          审计日志\n173\t        </span>\n174\t      ),\n175\t      children: (\n176\t        <AuditLogViewer logs={auditLogs} loading={auditLogLoading} />\n177\t      ),\n178\t    },\n179\t  ];\n180\t\n181\t  return (\n182\t    <div style={{ padding: 0 }}>\n183\t      {/* 页面标题 */}\n184\t      <div\n185\t        style={{\n186\t          marginBottom: 24,\n187\t          display: 'flex',\n188\t          justifyContent: 'space-between',\n189\t          alignItems: 'flex-start',\n190\t        }}\n191\t      >\n192\t        <div>\n193\t          <Title level={2} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>\n194\t            <RobotOutlined style={{ marginRight: 8, color: colors.purple[500] }} />\n195\t            AI Agent 管理\n196\t          </Title>\n197\t          <Paragraph type=\"secondary\">\n198\t            管理 AI Agent 配置、执行任务、查看审计日志\n199\t          </Paragraph>\n200\t        </div>\n201\t        <Button\n202\t          icon={<ReloadOutlined />}\n203\t          onClick={loadAgents}\n204\t          loading={loading}\n205\t        >\n206\t          刷新\n207\t        </Button>\n208\t      </div>\n209\t\n210\t      {/* 功能标签页 */}\n211\t      <Card\n212\t        style={{\n213\t          borderRadius: 12,\n214\t          boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.06), 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)',\n215\t        }}\n216\t      >\n217\t        <Tabs\n218\t selectedAgent?.id}\n242\t          </span>\n243\t        }\n244\t        open={executeModalOpen}\n245\t        onCancel={() => setExecuteModalOpen(false)}\n246\t        footer={null}\n247\t        width={600}\n248\t      >\n249\t        <Form form={form} layout=\"vertical\" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>\n250\t          <Form.Item\n251\t            label=\"输入参数 (JSON)\"\n252\t placeholder='{\"key\": \"value\"}'\n258\t              style={{ fontFamily: 'monospace' }}\n259\t            />\n260\t          </Form.Item>\n261\t          <Form.Item>\n262\t            <Button\n263\t              type=\"primary\"\n264\t              icon={<PlayCircleOutlined />}\n265\t              onClick={handleExecuteSubmit}\n266\t              loading={executing}\n267\t perPage?: number;\n60\t}\n61\t\n62\texport class ExecutionService {\n63\t  private executionRepo: ChatOpsExecutionRepository;\n64\t  private sessionRepo: ChatOpsSessionRepository;\n65\t  private auditRepo: ChatOpsAuditLogRepository;\n66\t  private commandService: CommandService;\n67\t  private eventBus?: EventBusService;\n68\t  /** 命令路由器 (可选，用于路由到真实命令处理器) */\n69\t  private commandRouter?: ChatOpsExecution {\n94\t    return {\n95\t      id: entity.id,\n96\t      commandId: entity.commandId,\n97\t      userId: entity.userId,\n98\t      platform: entity.platform,\n99\t      channel: entity.channel,\n100\t      params: entity.params,\n101\t      status: entity.status as ChatOpsExecutionStatus,\n102\t      startTime: entity.startTime,\n103\t      endTime: entity.endTime,\n104\t this.commandService.getByName(input.commandId);\n159\t\n160\t    const endTime = new Date();\n161\t    try {\n162\t      let executionResult: Record<string, unknown>;\n163\t\n164\t      // 如果有 commandRouter，通过路由器执行命令；否则使用 mock 行为 (向后兼容)\n165\t      if (this.commandRouter) {\n166\t        executionResult = await this.commandRouter.routeAndExecute(\n167\t          input.commandId,\n168\t          input.params ?? {},\n169\t        );\n170\t      } else {\n171\t        // 向后兼容: 保持原有 mock 行为\n172\t        executionResult = {\n173\t          output: `Command ${input.commandId} executed successfully`,\n174\t          exitCode: 0,\n175\t          durationMs: endTime.getTime() - execution.startTime.getTime(),\n176\t        };\n177\t      }\n178\t\n179\t      await this.executionRepo.updateStatus(\n180\t        execution.id,\n181\t        'completed',\n182\t        endTime,\n183\t        executionResult,\n184\t      );\n185\t\n186\t      await this.executionRepo.update(execution.id, {\n187\t        milestones: {\n188\t          started: execution.startTime.toISOString(),\n189\t          completed: endTime.toISOString(),\n190\t        },\n191\t      } as any);\n192\t\n193\t      // Create audit log\n194\t      await this.createAuditLog({\n195\t        traceId: execution.id,\n196\t        actor: { userId: input.userId, platform: input.platform },\n197\t        action: { command: input.commandId, params: input.params, channel: input.channel },\n198\t        result: 'success',\n199\t        context: { executionId: execution.id },\n200\t      });\n201\t    } catch (err) {\n202\t      await this.executionRepo.updateStatus(\n203\t        execution.id,\n204\t        'failed',\n205\t        endTime,\n206\t        {\n207\t          error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error',\n208\t          exitCode: 1,\n209\t        },\n210\t      );\n211\t\n212\t      await this.createAuditLog({\n213\t        traceId: execution.id,\n214\t        actor: { userId: input.userId, platform: input.platform },\n215\t        action: { command: input.commandId, params: input.params },\n216\t        result: 'failed',\n217\t        context: { executionId: execution.id, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown' },\n218\t      });\n219\t    }\n220\t\n221\t    // ARCH-004: 事件发布添加完整错误处理\n222\t    const eventResult = await this.publishExecutionEvent('chatops.execution.completed', {\n223\t      executionId: execution.id,\n224\t      commandId: execution.commandId,\n225\t      status: 'completed',\n226\t      userId: input.userId,\n227\t      platform: input.platform,\n228\t    });\n229\t\n230\t    // ARCH-004: 记录事件发布失败（但不阻塞执行）\n231\t    if (!eventResult.success) {\n232\t      console.warn('[ExecutionService] Event publish failed:', eventResult.error);\n233\t      if (eventResult.fallback) {\n234\t        console.log('[ExecutionService] Event persisted for fallback retry, eventId:', eventResult.eventId);\n235\t      }\n236\t    }\n237\t\n238\t    // Return updated execution from DB\n239\t    const updated = await this.executionRepo.findById(execution.id);\n240\t    return this.entityToExecution(updated!);\n241\t  }\n242\t\n243\t  /**\n244\t   * ARCH-004: 安全发布事件，支持 fallback 模式\n245\t   */\n246\t  private async publishExecutionEvent(\n247\t    eventType: string,\n248\t    payload: Record<string, unknown>,\n249\t  ): Promise<EventPublishResult> {\n250\t    if (!this.eventBus) {\n251\t      return {\n252\t        success: false,\n253\t        error: 'EventBus not available',\n254\t      };\n255\t    }\n256\t\n257\t    try {\n258\t      const eventId = await this.eventBus.publish(eventType, payload, {\n259\t        source: 'execution-service',\n260\t        publishedBy: 'ExecutionService',\n261\t      });\n262\t\n263\t      // ARCH-004: 检查是否为 fallback 发布\n264\t      const isFallback = eventId.startsWith('fallback:');\n265\t      return {\n266\t        success: true,\n267\t        eventId,\n268\t        fallback: isFallback,\n269\t      };\n270\t    } catch (err: unknown) {\n271\t      // ARCH-004: 区分错误类型\n272\t      const errorMsg = err instanceof EventBusError\n273\t        ? `${err.code}: ${err.message}`\n274\t        : (err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error');\n275\t\n276\t      return {\n277\t        success: false,\n278\t        error: errorMsg,\n279\t        fallback: err instanceof EventBusError && err.recoverable,\n280\t      };\n281\t    }\n282\t  }\n283\t\n284\t  async getById(id: string): Promise<ChatOpsExecution | undefined> {\n285\t    const entity = await this.executionRepo.findById(id);\n286\t    return entity ? this.entityToExecution(entity) : undefined;\n287\t  }\n288\t\n289\t  async list(filter: ChatOpsExecutionListFilter = {}): Promise<{ executions: ChatOpsExecution[]; total: number }> {\n290\t    let {\n295\t      entities = await this.executionRepo.findByUser(filter.userId);\n296\t    } else if (filter.status) {\n297\t      entities = await this.executionRepo.findByStatus(filter.status);\n298\t    } else {\n299\t      const result = await this.executionRepo.findAll({ limit: 1000 });\n300\t      entities = result.entities;\n301\t    }\n302\t\n303\t    if (filter.platform) {\n304\t      entities = entities.filter(e => e.platform === filter.platform);\n305\t    }\n306\t\n307\t    const total = entities.length;\n308\t    const page = filter.page ?? 1;\n309\t    const perPage = filter.perPage ?? this.entityToExecution(e)), total };\n314\t  }\n315\t\n316\t  // ==================== Session ====================\n317\t\n318\t  async createSession(input: ChatOpsSessionCreateInput): Promise<ChatOpsSession> {\n319\t    const session = createChatOpsSession(input);\n320\t\n321\t    await this.sessionRepo.insert({\n322\t      key: session.key,\n323\t      user_id: session.userId,\n324\t      channel_id: session.channelId,\n325\t      history: [],\n326\t      state: {},\n327\t    });\n328\t\n329\t    return session;\n330\t  }\n331\t\n332\t  async getSession(key: entity.key,\n337\t      userId: entity.userId,\n338\t      channelId: entity.channelId,\n339\t      history: entity.history,\n340\t      state: entity.state,\n341\t    };\n342\t  }\n343\t\n344\t  async updateSession(key: string, updates: { history?: Record<string, unknown>[]; state?: Record<string, unknown> }): Promise<ChatOpsSession | undefined> {\n345\t    const entity = await entity.history);\n349\t\n350\t    return {\n351\t      key,\n352\t      userId: entity.userId,\n353\t      channelId: entity.channelId,\n354\t      history: updates.history ?? entity.history,\n355\t      state: updates.state ?? entity.state,\n356\t    };\n357\t  }\n358\t\n359\t  // ==================== Audit ====================\n360\t\n361\t  private async createAuditLog(input: log.traceId,\n366\t      actor: log.actor,\n367\t      timestamp: log.timestamp,\n368\t      action: log.action,\n369\t      result: log.result,\n370\t      context: log.context,\n371\t    });\n372\t\n373\t    return this.entityToAuditLog(entity);\n374\t  }\n375\t\n376\t  async getAuditLogs(filter: ChatOpsAuditLogFilter = {}): Promise<{ logs: ChatOpsAuditLog[]; total: number }> {\n377\t    const result = await this.auditRepo.findAll({ limit: 1000, orderBy: 'timestamp', orderDir: 'DESC' });\n378\t    let items = result.entities;\n379\t\n380\t    if (filter.traceId) {\n381\t      items = items.filter(l => l.traceId === filter.traceId);\n382\t    }\n383\t    if (filter.actor) {\n384\t      items = items.filter(l =>\n385\t        (l.actor as Record<string, string>).userId?.includes(filter.actor!) ||\n386\t        JSON.stringify(l.actor).includes(filter.actor!)\n387\t      );\n388\t    }\n389\t    if (filter.result) {\n390\t      items = items.filter(l => l.result === filter.result);\n391\t    }\n392\t    if (filter.startDate) {\n393\t      items = items.filter(l => l.timestamp >= filter.startDate!);\n394\t    }\n395\t    if (filter.endDate) {\n396\t      items = items.filter(l => l.timestamp <= filter.endDate!);\n397\t    }\n398\t\n399\t    const total = items.length;\n400\t    const page = filter.page ?? 1;\n401\t    const perPage = filter.perPage ?? 20;\n402\t    const start = (page - 1) * perPage;\n403\t    const paginated = items.slice(start, start + perPage);\n404\t\n405\t    return { logs: paginated.map(e => this.entityToAuditLog(e)), total };\n406\t  }\n407\t\n408\t  async getAuditStats(): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {\n409\t    const totalCount = await this.auditRepo.countAll();\n410\t    const successCount = await this.auditRepo.countByResult('success');\n411\t    const failedCount = await this.auditRepo.countByResult('failed');\n412\t\n413\t    // Count by action type - fetch recent logs for action breakdown\n414\t    const recentLogs = await this.auditRepo.findRecent(24);\n415\t    const actionCounts: Record<string, number> = {};\n416\t    for (const log of recentLogs) {\n417\t      const action = log.action as Record<string, string>;\n418\t      const cmd = action.command || 'unknown';\n419\t      actionCounts[cmd] = (actionCounts[cmd] || 0) + 1;\n420\t    }\n421\t\n422\t    // Count by platform\n423\t    const platformCounts: Record<string, number> = {};\n424\t    for (const log of recentLogs) {\n425\t      const actor = log.actor as Record<string, string>;\n426\t      const platform = actor.platform || 'unknown';\n427\t      platformCounts[platform] = (platformCounts[platform] || 0) + 1;\n428\t    }\n429\t\n430\t    return {\n431\t      totalExecutions: totalCount,\n432\t      successCount,\n433\t      failedCount,\n434\t      successRate: totalCount > 0 ? (successCount / totalCount * 100).toFixed(2) + '%' : '0%',\n435\t      actionCounts,\n436\t      platformCounts,\n437\t    };\n438\t  }\n439\t\n440\t  async exportAuditLogs(filter: ChatOpsAuditLogFilter = {}): Promise<ChatOpsAuditLog[]> {\n441\t    const { logs } = await this.getAuditLogs(filter);\n442\t    return logs;\n443\t  }\n444\t}\n445\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. aliases: ['alerts'],\n80\t    permissionLevel: 'user',\n81\t    examples: ['/alert severity=critical hours=24'],\n82\t  },\n83\t];\n84\t\n85\t/**\n86\t * ChatOps Command Service - 解耦版本\n87\t * \n88\t * 使用新的解耦架构：\n89\t * - DBContext 替代直接数据库访问\n90\t * - ServiceRegistry 用于内部事件\n91\t * - EventBusService 用于外部事件\n92\t */\n93\texport class CommandService {\n94\t  private static _instance: options?.eventBus;\n103\t    if (!options?.repository) {\n104\t      this.commandRepository = new ChatOpsCommandRepository(this.pool);\n105\t    } else {\n106\t      this.commandRepository = options.repository;\n107\t    }\n108\t  }\n109\t\n110\t  /**\n111\t   * 获取单例\n112\t   */\n113\t  static getInstance(): CommandService {\n114\t    if (!CommandService._instance) {\n115\t      CommandService._instance = new CommandService();\n116\t    }\n117\t    return CommandService._instance;\n118\t  }\n119\t\n120\t  /**\n121\t   * 创建新实例（兼容旧代码）\n122\t   */\n123\t  static create(pool: DatabasePool, options?: { eventBus?: EventBusService; repository?: ChatOpsCommandRepository }): CommandService {\n124\t    return new CommandService({ pool, ...options });\n125\t  }\n126\t\n127\t  /**\n128\t   * 初始化服务\n129\t   */\n130\t  async initialize(): Promise<void> {\n131\t    if (this.initialized) return;\n132\t\n133\t    this.initialized = true;\n134\t\n135\t    console.log('[CommandService] Initialized');\n136\t  }\n137\t\n138\t  /** Seed default commands if the database is empty. Called once on startup. */\n139\t  async seedDefaults(): Promise<void> {\n140\t    if (!this.commandRepository) return;\n141\t\n142\t    const existing = await this.commandRepository.findAll({ limit: 1 });\n143\t    if (existing.total > 0) return; // Already seeded\n144\t\n145\t    for (const def of DEFAULT_COMMANDS) {\n146\t      try {\n147\t        await this.commandRepository.insert({\n148\t          name: def.name,\n149\t          subcommand: def.subcommand ??
