[],\n154\t        });\n155\t      } catch {\n156\t        // Skip if already exists (race condition or manual insert)\n157\t      }\n158\t    }\n159\t  }\n160\t\n161\t  // ==================== Command CRUD ====================\n162\t\n163\t  async insert(input: ChatOpsCommandCreateInput): Promise<ChatOpsCommand> {\n164\t    if (!this.commandRepository) {\n165\t      throw new Error('CommandService: no database repository configured');\n166\t    }\n167\t\n168\t    const entity = await this.commandRepository.insert({\n169\t      name: input.name,\n170\t      subcommand: input.subcommand ?? '',\n171\t      schema: input.schema ?? {},\n172\t      aliases: input.aliases ?? [],\n173\t      permissionLevel: input.permissionLevel ?? 'user',\n174\t      examples: input.examples ?? [],\n175\t    });\n176\t\n177\t    // 发布内部事件（解耦）\n178\t    // TODO: publish event when event bus is integrated\n179\t    // await registry.publish('chatops.command.created', {\n180\t    //   commandName: entity.name,\n181\t    //   permissionLevel: entity.permissionLevel,\n182\t    // });\n183\t\n184\t    // 同时通过 EventBus 发布外部事件\n185\t    await this.eventBus?.publish('chatops.command.created', {\n186\t      commandName: entity.name,\n187\t      permissionLevel: entity.permissionLevel,\n188\t    });\n189\t\n190\t    return this.entityToModel(entity);\n191\t  }\n192\t\n193\t  async getByName(name: string): Promise<ChatOpsCommand | undefined> {\n194\t    if (!this.commandRepository) {\n195\t      throw new Error('CommandService: no database repository configured');\n196\t    }\n197\t\n198\t    // Try direct name match\n199\t    let entity = await this.commandRepository.findByName(name);\n200\t    if (entity) return this.entityToModel(entity);\n201\t\n202\t    // Try alias match\n203\t    entity = await this.commandRepository.findByAlias(name);\n204\t    if (entity) return this.entityToModel(entity);\n205\t\n206\t    return undefined;\n207\t  }\n208\t\n209\t  async list(filter: ChatOpsCommandListFilter = {}): Promise<{ commands: ChatOpsCommand[]; total: number }> {\n210\t    if (!this.commandRepository) {\n211\t      throw new Error('CommandService: no database repository configured');\n212\t    }\n213\t\n214\t    let entities;\n215\t    if (filter.permissionLevel) {\n216\t      entities = await this.commandRepository.findByPermission(filter.permissionLevel);\n217\t    } else {\n218\t      const result = await this.commandRepository.findAll({ limit: 100, orderBy: 'name' });\n219\t      entities = result.entities;\n220\t    }\n221\t\n222\t    let commands = entities.map(e => this.entityToModel(e));\n223\t\n224\t    // Apply name filter (search name and aliases)\n225\t    if (filter.name) {\n226\t      const lower = filter.name.toLowerCase();\n227\t      commands = commands.filter(c =>\n228\t        c.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower) ||\n229\t        c.aliases.some(a => a.toLowerCase().includes(lower))\n230\t      );\n231\t    }\n232\t\n233\t    const total = commands.length;\n234\t    const page = filter.page ?? 1;\n235\t    const perPage = filter.perPage ?? 20;\n236\t    const start = (page - 1) * perPage;\n237\t    const paginated = commands.slice(start, start + perPage);\n238\t\n239\t    return { commands: paginated, total };\n240\t  }\n241\t\n242\t  async delete(name: string): Promise<boolean> {\n243\t    if (!this.commandRepository) {\n244\t      throw new Error('CommandService: no database repository configured');\n245\t    }\n246\t\n247\t    const entity = await this.commandRepository.findByName(name);\n248\t    if (!entity) return false;\n249\t\n250\t    const result = await this.commandRepository.delete(entity.id);\n251\t\n252\t    // TODO: publish event when event bus is integrated\n253\t    // if (result) {\n254\t    //   await registry.publish('chatops.command.deleted', { commandName: name });\n255\t    // }\n256\t\n257\t    return result;\n258\t  }\n259\t\n260\t  // ==================== Parsing & Help ====================\n261\t\n262\t  async getHelp(name: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown> | undefined> {\n263\t    const cmd = await this.getByName(name);\n264\t    if (!cmd) return undefined;\n265\t\n266\t    return {\n267\t      name: cmd.name,\n268\t      subcommand: cmd.subcommand,\n269\t      aliases: cmd.aliases,\n270\t      permissionLevel: cmd.permissionLevel,\n271\t      schema: cmd.schema,\n272\t      examples: cmd.examples,\n273\t    };\n274\t  }\n275\t\n276\t  async parseCommand(input: string): Promise<{ command: ChatOpsCommand | undefined; params: Record<string, string> }> {\n277\t    // Parse format: /command param1=value1 param2=value2\n278\t    const trimmed = input.trim();\n279\t    const parts = trimmed.split(/\\s+/);\n280\t\n281\t    let commandName = parts[0];\n282\t    // Remove leading slash\n283\t    if (commandName.startsWith('/')) {\n284\t      commandName = commandName.substring(1);\n285\t    }\n286\t\n287\t    const command = await this.getByName(commandName);\n288\t    const params: Record<string, string> = {};\n289\t\n290\t    // Parse key=value pairs\n291\t    for (let i = 1; i < parts.length; i++) {\n292\t      const match = parts[i].match(/^(\\w+)=(.+)$/);\n293\t      if (match) {\n294\t        params[match[1]] = match[2];\n295\t      }\n296\t    }\n297\t\n298\t    return { command, params };\n299\t  }\n300\t\n301\t  async getAllCommands(): Promise<ChatOpsCommand[]> {\n302\t    if (!this.commandRepository) {\n303\t      throw new Error('CommandService: no database repository configured');\n304\t    }\n305\t\n306\t    const result = await this.commandRepository.findAll({ limit: 1000 });\n307\t    return result.entities.map(e => this.entityToModel(e));\n308\t  }\n309\t\n310\t  /**\n311\t   * 获取健康状态\n312\t   */\n313\t  async getHealth(): Promise<{ status: 'healthy' | 'degraded'; latency: number }> {\n314\t    const start =   * P2-4: Unified JWT user extraction\n88\t   * All controller methods should use this instead of reading from body/query\n89\t   */\n90\t  protected getUser(request: FastifyRequest): { userId: {\n137\t    try {\n138\t      const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n139\t      // P2-4: Use unified getUser() helper\n140\t      const user = this.getUser(request);\n141\t      if (!user) {\n142\t        await reply.status(401).send({ success: false, error: 'UNAUTHORIZED' });\n143\t        return;\n144\t      }\n145\t      if (!body.command) {\n146\t        await exists\n154\t      const command = await this.commandService.getByName(body.command as string);\n155\t      if (!command) {\n156\t        await reply.status(404).send({ success: false, error: 'Command not found' });\n157\t        return;\n158\t      }\n159\t\n160\t      // Permission check via Capability engine (fail-closed)\n161\t      if (!this.permissionService) {\n162\t        await reply.status(503).send({\n163\t          success: false,\n164\t          error: 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE',\n165\t          message: '权限服务不可用，请稍后重试',\n166\t        });\n167\t        return;\n168\t      }\n169\t      const permissionResult = await this.permissionService.checkCommandPermission(\n170\t        user.userId,\n171\t        body.command as string,\n172\t        body.environment as string | undefined,\n173\t      );\n174\t      if (!permissionResult.allowed) {\n175\t        await reply.status(403).send({\n176\t          success: false,\n177\t          error: 'FORBIDDEN',\n178\t          reason: permissionResult.reason,\n179\t          requiresApproval: permissionResult.requiresApproval,\n180\t        });\n181\t        return;\n182\t      }\n183\t\n184\t      // Rate limit check\n185\t      if (this.rateLimitService) {\n186\t        const limitResult = await this.rateLimitService.checkLimit(\n187\t          user.userId,\n188\t          body.command as string,\n189\t        );\n190\t        if (!limitResult.allowed) {\n191\t          await reply.status(429).send({\n192\t            success: false,\n193\t            error: 'RATE_LIMITED',\n194\t            message: `命令 ${body.command} 触发速率限制`,\n195\t          });\n196\t          return;\n197\t        }\n198\t      }\n199\t\n200\t      const execution = await this.executionService.execute({\n201\t        commandId: body.command as string,\n202\t        userId: user.userId,\n203\t        platform: 'web',\n204\t        channel: body.channel as string ?? 'chatops-panel',\n205\t        params: body.params as Record<string, unknown> | undefined,\n206\t      });\n207\t\n208\t      await reply.status(201).send({ success: true, data: execution });\n209\t    } catch (err) {\n210\t      await reply.status(400).send({\n211\t        success: false,\n212\t        error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to execute command',\n213\t      });\n214\t    }\n215\t  }\n216\t\n217\t  async checkExecutionStatus(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply): Promise<void> {\n218\t    try {\n219\t      const params = request.params as { commandId: string };\n220\t      const execution = await this.executionService.getById(params.commandId);\n221\t      if (!execution) {\n222\t        await reply.status(404).send({ success: false, error: 'Execution not found' });\n223\t        return;\n224\t      }\n225\t      await reply.send({ success: true, data: execution });\n226\t    } catch (err) {\n227\t      await reply.status(500).send({\n228\t reply: FastifyReply): Promise<void> {\n235\t    try {\n236\t      // F-2: 添加认证检查\n237\t      const user = this.getUser(request);\n238\t      if (!user) {\n239\t        await reply.status(401).send({ success: false, error: 'UNAUTHORIZED' });\n240\t        return;\n241\t      }\n242\t      const query = request.query as Record<string, string | undefined>;\n243\t      // 若未指定 userId，使用当前用户的 userId\n244\t      const filterUserId = query.userId || user.userId;\n245\t      const { executions, total } = await this.executionService.list({\n246\t        commandId: query.commandId,\n247\t        userId: filterUserId,\n248\t        status: query.status as ChatOpsExecutionStatus,\n249\t        platform: query.platform,\n250\t        page: query.page ? parseInt(query.page) : undefined,\n251\t reply.status(500).send({\n256\t        success: false,\n257\t        error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Internal server error',\n258\t      });\n259\t    }\n260\t  }\n261\t\n262\t  // ==================== Webhook ====================\n263\t\n264\t  /**\n265\t   * 接收 IM 平台 Webhook 消息\n266\t   *\n267\t   * 流程：\n268\t   * 1. 飞书 Challenge 验证：特殊处理，返回 challenge\n269\t   * 2. 签名验证：生产环境强制验证，开发环境可跳过\n270\t   * 3. 验证失败返回 403\n271\t   * 4. 验证成功后从签名上下文中提取 userId（而非从 body 读）\n272\t   */\n273\t  async receiveMessage(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply): Promise<void> {\n274\t    try {\n275\t      const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n276\t      const query = (request.query ?? {}) as Record<string, string | undefined>;\n277\t      const headers: Record<string, string | undefined> = {};\n278\t      // 提取关键请求头\n279\t      const headerKeys = ['x-im-platform', 'X-IM-Platform'];\n280\t      for (const key of headerKeys) {\n281\t        const value = (request.headers as Record<string, string | undefined>)[key];\n282\t        if (value) headers[key] = value;\n283\t      }\n284\t\n285\t      // --- 飞书 Challenge 验证（URL 配置阶段） ---\n286\t      if (isFeishuChallenge(body)) {\n287\t        if (!WebhookVerifier.shouldVerify()) {\n288\t          // 开发/测试环境：直接返回 challenge\n289\t          await reply.send({ challenge: body.challenge });\n290\t          return;\n291\t        }\n292\t        // 生产环境：需通过 token 验证\n293\t        const result = WebhookVerifier.verifyFeishu(body);\n294\t        if (!result.valid) {\n295\t          await reply.status(403).send({\n296\t            success: false,\n297\t            error: result.error,\n298\t          });\n299\t          return;\n300\t        }\n301\t        await reply.send({ challenge: body.challenge });\n302\t        return;\n303\t      }\n304\t\n305\t      // --- 签名验证 ---\n306\t      if (WebhookVerifier.shouldVerify()) {\n307\t        const result = WebhookVerifier.verify(body, query, headers);\n308\t        if (!result.valid) {\n309\t          await reply.status(403).send({\n310\t            success: false,\n311\t            error: `Webhook signature verification failed: ${result.error}`,\n312\t          });\n313\t          return;\n314\t        }\n315\t\n316\t        // 验证成功，使用签名上下文中的 userId\n317\t        const userId = result.userId || 'webhook-anonymous';\n318\t        const platform = result.platform;\n319\t        const channel = (body.channel || 'default') as string;\n320\t\n321\t        // Parse incoming IM message\n322\t        const text = (body.text || body.message || '') as string;\n323\t        const { command: parsedCmd, params } = await this.commandService.parseCommand(text);\n324\t\n325\t        if (!parsedCmd) {\n326\t          await reply.status(400).send({\n327\t            success: false,\n328\t            error: 'Unknown command. Use /help for available commands.',\n329\t          });\n330\t          return;\n331\t        }\n332\t\n333\t        // Permission check via Capability engine (webhook path, fail-closed)\n334\t        if (!this.permissionService) {\n335\t          await reply.status(503).send({\n336\t            success: false,\n337\t            error: 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE',\n338\t            message: '权限服务不可用',\n339\t          });\n340\t          return;\n341\t        }\n342\t        const permissionResult = await this.permissionService.checkCommandPermission(\n343\t          userId,\n344\t          parsedCmd.name,\n345\t          body.environment as string | undefined,\n346\t        );\n347\t        if (!permissionResult.allowed) {\n348\t          await reply.status(403).send({\n349\t            success: false,\n350\t            error: 'FORBIDDEN',\n351\t            reason: permissionResult.reason,\n352\t          });\n353\t          return;\n354\t        }\n355\t\n356\t        // Rate limit check (webhook path)\n357\t        if (this.rateLimitService) {\n358\t          const limitResult = await this.rateLimitService.checkLimit(userId, parsedCmd.name);\n359\t          if (!limitResult.allowed) {\n360\t            await reply.status(429).send({\n361\t              success: false,\n362\t              error: 'RATE_LIMITED',\n363\t              message: `命令 ${parsedCmd.name} 触发速率限制`,\n364\t            });\n365\t            return;\n366\t          }\n367\t        }\n368\t\n369\t        const execution = await this.executionService.execute({\n370\t          commandId: parsedCmd.name,\n371\t          userId,\n372\t          platform,\n373\t          channel,\n374\t          params: params as Record<string, unknown>,\n375\t        });\n376\t\n377\t        await reply.status(201).send({ success: true, data: execution, command: parsedCmd });\n378\t      } else {\n379\t        // 开发/测试环境：从 JWT middleware 获取 userId，不允许从 body 伪造\n380\t        const user = (request as any).user as { userId: string } | undefined;\n381\t        const userId = user?.userId;\n382\t        if (!userId) {\n383\t          await reply.status(401).send({\n384\t            success: false,\n385\t            error: 'Webhook 未配置认证信息',\n386\t          });\n387\t          return;\n388\t        }\n389\t        const platform = (body.platform || 'webhook') as string;\n390\t { command: parsedCmd, params } = await this.commandService.parseCommand(text);\n395\t\n396\t        if (!parsedCmd) {\n397\t          await reply.status(400).send({\n398\t            success: const logs = await this.executionService.exportAuditLogs({\n505\t        traceId: body?.traceId as string | undefined,\n506\t        actor: body?.actor as string | undefined,\n507\t        result: await reply.send({ success: true, data: recommendations, total: recommendations.length });\n537\t    } catch (err) {\n538\t      await reply.status(500).send({\n539\t        success: false,\n540\t ====================\n546\t\n547\t  async getSessionMessages(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply): Promise<void> {\n548\t    try {\n549\t      const params = request.params as { id: string };\n550\t      const query = request.query as { limit?: string; cursor?: string };\n551\t      const { messages, hasMore } = await this.messageRepo.findBySession(params.id, {\n552\t        limit: query.limit ? parseInt(query.limit) : 50,\n553\t        cursor: query.cursor,\n554\t      });\n555\t      await reply.send({\n556\t        success: true,\n557\t        data: messages,\n558\t        hasMore,\n559\t        nextCursor: hasMore ? messages[messages.length - 1]?.createdAt.toISOString() : null,\n560\t      });\n561\t    } catch (err) {\n562\t      await reply.status(500).send({\n563\t 1a) ====================\n570\t\n571\t  async streamRecommendations(\n572\t    request: FastifyRequest,\n573\t    reply: FastifyReply,\n574\t    connectionManager: SSEConnectionManager | null,\n575\t    eventSubscriber: ChatOpsEventSubscriber | null,\n576\t  ): Promise<void> {\n577\t    const user = (request as any).user as { userId: string } | undefined;\n578\t    if (!user) {\n579\t      await reply.code(401).send({ error: 'UNAUTHORIZED' });\n580\t      return;\n581\t    }\n582\t\n583\t    // SSE headers\n584\t    reply.header('Content-Type', 'text/event-stream');\n585\t    reply.header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache');\n586\t    reply.header('Connection', 'keep-alive');\n587\t    reply.header('X-Accel-Buffering', 'no');\n588\t\n589\t    // Send connection confirmation\n590\t    try {\n591\t      reply.raw.write('event: connected\\ndata: {\"status\":\"ok\"}\\n\\n');\n592\t    } catch {\n593\t      return;\n594\t    }\n595\t\n596\t    // Send initial snapshot\n597\t    const initialRecs = eventSubscriber?.getActiveRecommendations() ?? [];\n598\t    try {\n599\t      reply.raw.write(\n600\t        `event: recommendations\\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ recommendations: initialRecs })}\\n\\n`,\n601\t      );\n602\t    } catch {\n603\t      return;\n604\t    }\n605\t\n606\t    // Set up listener for updates\n607\t    const connId = `${user.userId}:${Date.now()}`;\n608\t    const listener = (data: Record<string, unknown>) => {\n609\t      const raw = (reply as any).raw;\n610\t      if (raw?.writableEnded) {\n611\t        connectionManager?.removeConnection(connId);\n612\t        return;\n613\t      }\n614\t      try {\n615\t        raw.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\\n\\n`);\n616\t      } catch {\n617\t        connectionManager?.removeConnection(connId);\n618\t      }\n619\t    };\n620\t\n621\t    // Register with connection manager for heartbeat + event forwarding\n622\t    connectionManager?.addConnection(\n623\t      { id: connId, userId: user.userId, listener, connectedAt: new Date() },\n624\t      reply,\n625\t    );\n626\t\n627\t    // 安全修复: H-NEW-1 - 客户端断开时立即清理 listener，不等待 30s 心跳\n628\t    reply.raw.on('close', () => {\n629\t      connectionManager?.removeConnection(connId);\n630\t    });\n631\t  }\n632\t\n633\t  // ==================== Notification Preferences (Phase 1a) ====================\n634\t\n635\t  async getNotificationPreferences(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply): Promise<void> {\n636\t    try {\n637\t      const user = this.getUser(request);\n638\t      if (!user) {\n639\t        await reply.status(401).send({ success: false, error: 'UNAUTHORIZED' });\n640\t        return;\n641\t      }\n642\t      const prefs = await this.notifPrefService.listByUserId(user.userId);\n643\t      await reply.send({ success: true, data: prefs });\n644\t    } catch (err) {\n645\t      await reply.status(500).send({\n646\t        success: false,\n647\t        error: err instanceof Error ? {\n986\t        state: 'disabled',\n987\t        message: 'EventBus not initialized',\n988\t        natsAvailable: false,\n989\t        reconnectAttempts: 0,\n990\t      };\n991\t\n992\t      const sseConnections = this.eventSubscriber?.isFallbackMode() ? * Command Router — 命令路由分发服务\n3\t *\n4\t * B-4: 将 ChatOps 命令分发到对应的业务服务\n5\t * Phase 1: 目标服务不存在时返回 mock 结果 (标记 mock: true)\n6\t * Phase 2: 支持注册内置 handler (status/logs/help 等不需要外部集成的命令)\n7\t */\n8\t\n9\texport interface RouteTarget {\n10\t  service: string;\n11\t  method: string;\n12\t  paramsMapper?: (params: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown>;\n13\t}\n14\t\n15\t/** 命令处理器函数签名 */\n16\texport type CommandHandler = (\n17\t  params: Record<string, unknown>,\n18\t) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;\n19\t\n20\tconst COMMAND_ROUTES: Record<string, RouteTarget> = {\n21\t  'deploy': { service: 'deploy', method: 'deploy' },\n22\t  'rollback': { service: 'deploy', method: 'rollback' },\n23\t  'restart': { service: 'deploy', method: 'restartPod' },\n24\t  'status': { service: 'monitoring', method: 'getStatus' },\n25\t  'logs': { service: 'monitoring', method: 'getLogs' },\n26\t  'diagnose': { service: 'diagnostic', method: 'runDiagnosis' },\n27\t  'pipeline': { service: 'pipeline', method: 'getPipeline' },\n28\t  'selfhealing_trigger': { service: 'selfhealing', method: 'executePolicy' },\n29\t};\n30\t\n31\t/** 内置命令 (不需要外部服务集成的命令) */\n32\tconst BUILTIN_COMMANDS = ['status', 'logs', 'help', 'ping'];\n33\t\n34\texport class CommandRouter {\n35\t  private services: Map<string, any>;\n36\t  /** 用户注册的自定义处理器 */\n37\t  private handlers: Map<string, CommandHandler> = new Map();\n38\t\n39\t  constructor(services: Map<string, any>) {\n40\t    this.services = services;\n41\t    // 注册内置处理器\n42\t    this.registerBuiltinHandlers();\n43\t  }\n44\t\n45\t  /** 注册内置处理器 (status/logs/help/ping) */\n46\t  private registerBuiltinHandlers(): void {\n47\t    this.handlers.set('status', async (params: Record<string, unknown>) => ({\n48\t      status: 'ok',\n49\t      command: 'status',\n50\t      params,\n51\t      output: `资源状态查询完成`,\n52\t      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n53\t    }));\n54\t\n55\t    this.handlers.set('logs', async (params: Record<string, unknown>) => ({\n56\t      status: 'ok',\n57\t      command: 'logs',\n58\t      params,\n59\t      output: `日志查询完成`,\n60\t      lines: [],\n61\t      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n62\t    }));\n63\t\n64\t    this.handlers.set('help', async (params: Record<string, unknown>) => ({\n65\t      status: 'ok',\n66\t      command: 'help',\n67\t      params,\n68\t      output: '可用命令: deploy, rollback, restart, status, logs, diagnose, pipeline, selfhealing_trigger, help, ping',\n69\t      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n70\t    }));\n71\t\n72\t    this.handlers.set('ping', async () => ({\n73\t      status: 'ok',\n74\t      command: 'ping',\n75\t      output: 'pong',\n76\t      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n77\t    }));\n78\t  }\n79\t\n80\t  /**\n81\t   * 注册自定义命令处理器\n82\t   * @param name 命令名称\n83\t   * @param handler 处理器函数\n84\t   */\n85\t  registerHandler(name: string, handler: CommandHandler): void {\n86\t    this.handlers.set(name, handler);\n87\t  }\n88\t\n89\t  /**\n90\t   * 路由并执行命令\n91\t   * @param commandName 命令名称\n92\t   * @param params 命令参数\n93\t   * @returns 执行结果\n94\t   */\n95\t  async routeAndExecute(\n96\t    commandName: string,\n97\t    params: Record<string, unknown>,\n98\t  ): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {\n99\t    // 优先检查自定义处理器\n100\t    const handler = this.handlers.get(commandName);\n101\t    if (handler) {\n102\t      return handler(params);\n103\t    }\n104\t\n105\t    // 回退到服务路由\n106\t    return this.route(commandName, params);\n107\t  }\n108\t\n109\t  async route(commandName: string, params: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {\n110\t    const target = COMMAND_ROUTES[commandName];\n111\t    if (!target) {\n112\t      // 未知命令: 返回友好错误\n113\t      throw new Error(`未知命令: ${commandName}。使用 /help 查看可用命令列表`);\n114\t    }\n115\t\n116\t    const service = this.services.get(target.service);\n117\t    if (!service) {\n118\t      // Phase 1 降级: 返回 mock 结果\n119\t      return this.mockResult(commandName, params, target.service);\n120\t    }\n121\t\n122\t    const mappedParams = target.paramsMapper?.(params) ?? params;\n123\t    return service[target.method](mappedParams);\n124\t  }\n125\t\n126\t  /** Phase 1 mock 结果 (目标服务不存在时返回) */\n127\t  private mockResult(\n128\t    commandName: string,\n129\t    params: Record<string, unknown>,\n130\t    serviceName: string,\n131\t  ): Record<string, unknown> {\n132\t    return {\n133\t      mock: true,\n134\t      command: commandName,\n135\t      params,\n136\t      message: `服务 ${serviceName} 尚未接入，此为模拟结果`,\n137\t      output: `[Mock] 命令 ${commandName} 执行完成`,\n138\t      status: 'completed',\n139\t      pendingIntegration: BUILTIN_COMMANDS.includes(commandName) ? false : true,\n140\t    };\n141\t  }\n142\t}\n143\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be * Permission Service — 双层权限校验\n3\t *\n4\t * SE-7 修复: ROLE_PERMISSIONS 从数据库查询替代硬编码\n5\t *\n6\t * 功能:\n7\t * 1. 命令级权限 (命令 → 权限点映射硬编码，角色权限从数据库查)\n8\t * 2. 资源级权限 (复用 RBAC user_resources 表)\n9\t * 3. 权限结果审计日志写入\n10\t */\n11\t\n12\timport { DatabasePool } from '../database';\n13\t\n14\texport interface PermissionCheckResult {\n15\t  allowed: boolean;\n16\t  reason?: string;\n17\t  deniedAt?: 'command_level' | 'resource_level';\n18\t}\n19\t\n20\t// 命令 → 权限点映射 (业务逻辑，保留硬编码)\n21\tconst COMMAND_PERMISSION: Record<string, string> = {\n22\t  'deploy': 'chatops:deploy',\n23\t  'rollback': 'chatops:deploy',\n24\t  'restart': 'chatops:restart',\n25\t  'logs': 'chatops:read',\n26\t  'status': 'chatops:read',\n27\t  'diagnose': 'chatops:diagnose',\n28\t  'pipeline': 'chatops:read',\n29\t  'selfhealing_trigger': 'chatops:diagnose',\n30\t};\n31\t\n32\texport class PermissionService {\n33\t  // 角色权限缓存 (带 TTL)\n34\t  private rolePermsCache: Map<string, { perms: string[]; expiresAt: number }> = new Map();\n35\t  // SE-7 修复: TTL 从 60 秒降低到 10 秒，减少缓存不一致窗口\n36\t  // 注意: 当 PermissionService 被实际使用时，需在 RBAC 变更处调用 invalidateCache()\n37\t  private readonly CACHE_TTL_MS = 10_000; // 10 秒\n38\t\n39\t  constructor(private pool: DatabasePool) {}\n40\t\n41\t  /**\n42\t   * 从数据库查询角色权限 (带缓存)\n43\t   * SE-7 修复: 不再使用硬编码 ROLE_PERMISSIONS\n44\t   */\n45\t  async getRolePermissions(roleName: string): Promise<string[]> {\n46\t    const cached = this.rolePermsCache.get(roleName);\n47\t    if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) {\n48\t      return cached.perms;\n49\t    }\n50\t\n51\t    try {\n52\t      const result = await this.pool.query(\n53\t        `SELECT DISTINCT p.resource, p.action\n54\t         FROM role_permissions rp\n55\t         JOIN permissions p ON rp.permission_id = p.id\n56\t         JOIN roles r ON rp.role_id = r.id\n57\t         WHERE r.name = $1`,\n58\t        [roleName],\n59\t      );\n60\t\n61\t      const perms = result.rows.map((row: { resource: string; action: string }) => `${row.resource}:${row.action}`);\n62\t      this.rolePermsCache.set(roleName, { perms, expiresAt: Date.now() + this.CACHE_TTL_MS });\n63\t      return perms;\n64\t    } catch (err) {\n65\t      console.warn(`[PermissionService] Failed to query permissions for role ${roleName}:`, err);\n66\t      // 降级: 返回空权限 (拒绝所有)\n67\t      return [];\n68\t    }\n69\t  }\n70\t\n71\t  /** Step 1: 命令级权限 */\n72\t  async checkCommandLevel(userId: string, userRole: string, command: string): Promise<PermissionCheckResult> {\n73\t    const requiredPerm = COMMAND_PERMISSION[command];\n74\t    if (!requiredPerm) {\n75\t      return { allowed: false, reason: '命令不存在', deniedAt: 'command_level' };\n76\t    }\n77\t\n78\t    const rolePerms = await this.getRolePermissions(userRole);\n79\t    if (!rolePerms.includes(requiredPerm)) {\n80\t      return { allowed: false, reason: `缺少权限: ${requiredPerm}`, deniedAt: 'command_level' };\n81\t    }\n82\t\n83\t    return { allowed: true };\n84\t  }\n85\t\n86\t  /** Step 2: 资源级权限 */\n87\t  async checkResourceLevel(userId: string, resourceType: string, resourceId: string, action?: string): Promise<PermissionCheckResult> {\n88\t    try {\n89\t      const conditions = ['user_id = $1', 'resource_type = $2', 'resource_id = $3'];\n90\t      const params: any[] = [userId, resourceType, resourceId];\n91\t\n92\t      if (action) {\n93\t        conditions.push(`action = $${params.length + 1}`);\n94\t        params.push(action);\n95\t      }\n96\t\n97\t      const result = await this.pool.query(\n98\t        `SELECT 1 FROM user_resources\n99\t         WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}\n100\t         LIMIT 1`,\n101\t        params,\n102\t      );\n103\t\n104\t      if (result.rowCount === 0) {\n105\t        return { allowed: false, reason: `无权访问资源: ${resourceType}/${resourceId}`, deniedAt: 'resource_level' };\n106\t      }\n107\t\n108\t      return { allowed: true };\n109\t    } catch (err) {\n110\t      console.warn('[PermissionService] Resource level check failed:', err);\n111\t      return { allowed: false, reason: '资源权限校验失败', deniedAt: 'resource_level' };\n112\t    }\n113\t  }\n114\t\n115\t  /**\n116\t   * Capability 权限检查 — 用于 ChatOps 命令执行前校验\n117\t   * 检查用户是否有权限执行某个命令（通过 Capability 映射）\n118\t   */\n119\t  async checkCommandPermission(userId: string, command: string, environment?: string): Promise<{\n120\t    allowed: boolean;\n121\t    capability?: string;\n122\t    riskLevel?: number;\n123\t    requiresApproval?: boolean;\n124\t    reason?: string;\n125\t  }> {\n126\t    try {\n127\t      // 1. 获取命令的 Capability 映射\n128\t      const mappingResult = await this.pool.query(\n129\t        `SELECT cm.*, ac.enabled, ac.approvers, ac.threshold\n130\t         FROM chatops_capability_mappings cm\n131\t         LEFT JOIN chatops_approval_configs ac ON cm.capability_id = ac.capability\n132\t         WHERE cm.command_id = $1 AND (cm.environment = $2 OR cm.environment IS NULL)\n133\t         ORDER BY cm.environment DESC NULLS LAST\n134\t         LIMIT 1`,\n135\t        [command, environment || null]\n136\t      );\n137\t\n138\t      if (!mappingResult.rows[0]) {\n139\t        return { allowed: false, reason: '命令未配置能力映射' };\n140\t      }\n141\t\n142\t      const mapping = mappingResult.rows[0];\n143\t\n144\t      // 2. 获取用户角色\n145\t      const userRolesResult = await this.pool.query(\n146\t        `SELECT r.name FROM roles r\n147\t         JOIN user_roles ur ON r.id = ur.role_id\n148\t         WHERE ur.user_id = $1`,\n149\t        [userId]\n150\t      );\n151\t\n152\t      if (userRolesResult.rows.length === 0) {\n153\t        return { allowed: false, reason: '用户无角色' };\n154\t      }\n155\t\n156\t      // 3. 检查用户角色是否有该 capability 的权限\n157\t      const roleNames = userRolesResult.rows.map((r: any) => r.name);\n158\t      const permResult = await this.pool.query(\n159\t        `SELECT 1 FROM chatops_command_permissions cp\n160\t         JOIN chatops_command_role_assignments cra ON cp.id = cra.command_permission_id\n161\t         JOIN chatops_roles r ON cra.role_id = r.id\n162\t         WHERE cp.capability = $1 AND r.name = ANY($2)\n163\t         LIMIT 1`,\n164\t        [mapping.capability_id, roleNames]\n165\t      );\n166\t\n167\t      if (permResult.rowCount === 0) {\n168\t        // 检查是否为超管角色\n169\t        if (roleNames.includes('admin') || roleNames.includes('super_admin')) {\n170\t          return {\n171\t            allowed: true,\n172\t            capability: mapping.capability_id,\n173\t            riskLevel: mapping.risk_level,\n174\t            requiresApproval: mapping.requires_approval,\n175\t          };\n176\t        }\n177\t        return {\n178\t          allowed: false,\n179\t          capability: mapping.capability_id,\n180\t          riskLevel: mapping.risk_level,\n181\t          requiresApproval: mapping.requires_approval,\n182\t          reason: `缺少 Capability 权限: ${mapping.capability_id}`,\n183\t        };\n184\t      }\n185\t\n186\t      return {\n187\t        allowed: true,\n188\t        capability: mapping.capability_id,\n189\t        riskLevel: mapping.risk_level,\n190\t        requiresApproval: mapping.requires_approval,\n191\t      };\n192\t    } catch (err) {\n193\t      console.warn('[PermissionService] Capability check failed:', err);\n194\t      return { allowed: false, reason: 'Capability 权限检查失败' };\n195\t    }\n196\t  }\n197\t\n198\t  /**\n199\t   * 获取用户可执行的命令列表\n200\t   */\n201\t  async getUserAllowedCommands(userId: string): Promise<string[]> {\n202\t    try {\n203\t      const userRolesResult = await this.pool.query(\n204\t        `SELECT r.name FROM roles r\n205\t         JOIN user_roles ur ON r.id = ur.role_id\n206\t         WHERE ur.user_id = $1`,\n207\t        [userId]\n208\t      );\n209\t\n210\t      if (userRolesResult.rows.length === 0) return [];\n211\t\n212\t      const roleNames = userRolesResult.rows.map((r: any) => r.name);\n213\t\n214\t      // 超管可执行所有命令\n215\t      if (roleNames.includes('admin') || roleNames.includes('super_admin')) {\n216\t        const allResult = await this.pool.query('SELECT DISTINCT command_id FROM chatops_capability_mappings');\n217\t        return allResult.rows.map((r: any) => r.command_id);\n218\t      }\n219\t\n220\t      const result = await this.pool.query(\n221\t        `SELECT DISTINCT cp.command FROM chatops_command_permissions cp\n222\t         JOIN chatops_command_role_assignments cra ON cp.id = cra.command_permission_id\n223\t         JOIN chatops_roles r ON cra.role_id = r.id\n224\t         WHERE r.name = ANY($1)`,\n225\t        [roleNames]\n226\t      );\n227\t\n228\t      return result.rows.map((r: any) => r.command);\n229\t    } catch {\n230\t      return [];\n231\t    }\n232\t  }\n233\t\n234\t  /**\n235\t   * 串联校验: 命令级 → 资源级\n236\t   * 校验结果自动写入审计日志\n237\t   */\n238\t  async check(\n239\t    userId: string,\n240\t    userRole: string,\n241\t    command: string,\n242\t    resourceType?: string,\n243\t    resourceId?: string,\n244\t  ): Promise<PermissionCheckResult> {\n245\t    // Step 1: 命令级\n246\t    const cmdResult = await this.checkCommandLevel(userId, userRole, command);\n247\t    if (!cmdResult.allowed) {\n248\t      await this.writeAuditLog(userId, command, cmdResult, resourceType, resourceId);\n249\t      return cmdResult;\n250\t    }\n251\t\n252\t    // Step 2: 资源级 (可选)\n253\t    if (resourceType && resourceId) {\n254\t      const resResult = await this.checkResourceLevel(userId, resourceType, resourceId);\n255\t      await this.writeAuditLog(userId, command, resResult, resourceType, resourceId);\n256\t      return resResult;\n257\t    }\n258\t\n259\t    return { allowed: true };\n260\t  }\n261\t\n262\t  /** 清除缓存 (在权限变更后调用) */\n263\t  invalidateCache(roleName?: string): void {\n264\t    if (roleName) {\n265\t      this.rolePermsCache.delete(roleName);\n266\t    } else {\n267\t      this.rolePermsCache.clear();\n268\t    }\n269\t  }\n270\t\n271\t  /** 写入审计日志 */\n272\t  private async writeAuditLog(\n273\t    userId: string,\n274\t    command: string,\n275\t    result: PermissionCheckResult,\n276\t    resourceType?: string,\n277\t    resourceId?: string,\n278\t  ): Promise<void> {\n279\t    try {\n280\t      await this.pool.query(\n281\t        `INSERT INTO chatops_audit_logs (actor, command, result, resource_type, resource_id, timestamp)\n282\t         VALUES ($1::jsonb, $2, $3, $4, $5, NOW())`,\n283\t        [\n284\t          JSON.stringify({ userId }),\n285\t          command,\n286\t          result.allowed ? 'allowed' : `denied: ${result.reason}`,\n287\t          resourceType || null,\n288\t          resourceId || null,\n289\t        ],\n290\t      );\n291\t    } catch (err) {\n292\t      console.warn('[PermissionService] Failed to write audit log:', err);\n293\t    }\n294\t  }\n295\t\n296\t  // ==================== Role Management CRUD ====================\n297\t\n298\t  async getAllRoles(): Promise<any[]> {\n299\t    const roles = await this.pool.query(\n300\t      `SELECT r.id, r.name, r.description, r.created_at, r.updated_at,\n301\t              COUNT(DISTINCT cp.id) as command_count,\n302\t              COUNT(DISTINCT ur.user_id) as user_count\n303\t       FROM chatops_roles r\n304\t       LEFT JOIN chatops_command_role_assignments cra ON r.id = cra.role_id\n305\t       LEFT JOIN chatops_command_permissions cp ON cra.command_permission_id = cp.id\n306\t       LEFT JOIN chatops_user_roles ur ON r.id = ur.role_id\n307\t       GROUP BY r.id\n308\t       ORDER BY r.name`\n309\t    );\n310\t\n311\t    const results: any[] = [];\n312\t    for (const row of roles.rows) {\n313\t      const perms = await this.pool.query(\n314\t        'SELECT permission FROM chatops_role_permissions WHERE role_id = $1 ORDER BY permission',\n315\t        [row.id]\n316\t      );\n317\t      results.push({\n318\t        ...row,\n319\t        permissions: perms.rows.map((r: any) => r.permission),\n320\t      });\n321\t    }\n322\t    return results;\n323\t  }\n324\t\n325\t  async getRoleById(id: string): Promise<any | null> {\n326\t    const result = await this.pool.query('SELECT * FROM chatops_roles WHERE id = $1', [id]);\n327\t    if (!result.rows[0]) return null;\n328\t    const perms = await this.pool.query(\n329\t      'SELECT permission FROM chatops_role_permissions WHERE role_id = $1',\n330\t      [id]\n331\t    );\n332\t    return { ...result.rows[0], permissions: perms.rows.map((r: any) => r.permission) };\n333\t  }\n334\t\n335\t  async createRole(input: { name: string; description?: string; permissions?: string[] }): Promise<any> {\n336\t    const { v4: uuidv4 } = await import('uuid');\n337\t    const id = uuidv4();\n338\t    const now = new Date();\n339\t\n340\t    await this.pool.query(\n341\t      `INSERT INTO chatops_roles (id, name, description, created_at, updated_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,\n342\t      [id, input.name, input.description || '', now, now]\n343\t    );\n344\t\n345\t    if (input.permissions?.length) {\n346\t      for (const perm of input.permissions) {\n347\t        await this.pool.query(\n348\t          `INSERT INTO chatops_role_permissions (id, role_id, permission) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)`,\n349\t          [uuidv4(), id, perm]\n350\t        );\n351\t      }\n352\t    }\n353\t\n354\t    this.invalidateCache();\n355\t    return this.getRoleById(id);\n356\t  }\n357\t\n358\t  async updateRole(id: string, input: { name?: string; description?: string; permissions?: string[] }): Promise<any | null> {\n359\t    const existing = await this.getRoleById(id);\n360\t    if (!existing) return null;\n361\t\n362\t    const { v4: uuidv4 } = await import('uuid');\n363\t    const updates: string[] = [];\n364\t    const params: any[] = [];\n365\t    let pi = 1;\n366\t\n367\t    if (input.name !== undefined) { updates.push(`name = $${pi++}`); params.push(input.name); }\n368\t    if (input.description !== undefined) { updates.push(`description = $${pi++}`); params.push(input.description); }\n369\t\n370\t    if (updates.length > 0) {\n371\t      updates.push(`updated_at = $${pi++}`);\n372\t      params.push(new Date(), id);\n373\t      await this.pool.query(\n374\t        `UPDATE chatops_roles SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = $${pi}`,\n375\t        params\n376\t      );\n377\t    }\n378\t\n379\t    if (input.permissions !== undefined) {\n380\t      await this.pool.query('DELETE FROM chatops_role_permissions WHERE role_id = $1', [id]);\n381\t      for (const perm of input.permissions) {\n382\t        await this.pool.query(\n383\t          `INSERT INTO chatops_role_permissions (id, role_id, permission) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)`,\n384\t          [uuidv4(), id, perm]\n385\t        );\n386\t      }\n387\t    }\n388\t\n389\t    this.invalidateCache();\n390\t    return this.getRoleById(id);\n391\t  }\n392\t\n393\t  async deleteRole(id: string): Promise<boolean> {\n394\t    const result = await this.pool.query('DELETE FROM chatops_roles WHERE id = $1', [id]);\n395\t    if (result.rowCount ?? 0 > 0) {\n396\t      this.invalidateCache();\n397\t      return true;\n398\t    }\n399\t    return false;\n400\t  }\n401\t\n402\t  // ==================== Command Permission CRUD ====================\n403\t\n404\t  async getAllCommandPermissions(): Promise<any[]> {\n405\t    const result = await this.pool.query(\n406\t      `SELECT cp.*,\n407\t              COALESCE(ARRAY_AGG(DISTINCT cra.role_id) FILTER (WHERE cra.role_id IS NOT NULL), ARRAY[]::text[]) as assigned_roles\n408\t       FROM chatops_command_permissions cp\n409\t       LEFT JOIN chatops_command_role_assignments cra ON cp.id = cra.command_permission_id\n410\t       GROUP BY cp.id\n411\t       ORDER BY cp.command`\n412\t    );\n413\t    return result.rows;\n414\t  }\n415\t\n416\t  async getCommandPermission(id: string): Promise<any | null> {\n417\t    const result = await this.pool.query(\n418\t      `SELECT cp.*,\n419\t              COALESCE(ARRAY_AGG(DISTINCT cra.role_id) FILTER (WHERE cra.role_id IS NOT NULL), ARRAY[]::text[]) as assigned_roles\n420\t       FROM chatops_command_permissions cp\n421\t       LEFT JOIN chatops_command_role_assignments cra ON cp.id = cra.command_permission_id\n422\t       WHERE cp.id = $1 GROUP BY cp.id`,\n423\t      [id]\n424\t    );\n425\t    return result.rows[0] || null;\n426\t  }\n427\t\n428\t  async createCommandPermission(input: {\n429\t    command: string; description?: string; capability: string;\n430\t    risk_level?: number; requires_approval?: boolean; role_ids?: string[];\n431\t  }): Promise<any> {\n432\t    const { v4: uuidv4 } = await import('uuid');\n433\t    const id = uuidv4();\n434\t    const now = new Date();\n435\t\n436\t    await this.pool.query(\n437\t      `INSERT INTO chatops_command_permissions (id, command, description, capability, risk_level, requires_approval, created_at, updated_at)\n438\t       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8)`,\n439\t      [id, input.command, input.description || '', input.capability,\n440\t       input.risk_level || 1, input.requires_approval || false, now, now]\n441\t    );\n442\t\n443\t    for (const rid of input.role_ids || []) {\n444\t      await }\n449\t\n450\t    return this.getCommandPermission(id);\n451\t  }\n452\t\n453\t  async updateCommandPermission(id: string, input: {\n454\t    description?: string; capability?: string; risk_level?: number;\n455\t    requires_approval?: boolean; role_ids?: string[];\n456\t  }): Promise<any | null> {\n457\t    const existing = await this.getCommandPermission(id);\n458\t    if (!existing) return null;\n459\t\n460\t    const { v4: uuidv4 } = await import('uuid');\n461\t    const updates: string[] = [];\n462\t    const params: any[] = [];\n463\t    let pi = 1;\n464\t\n465\t    if (input.description !== undefined) { updates.push(`description = $${pi++}`); params.push(input.description); }\n466\t    if (input.capability !== undefined) { updates.push(`capability = $${pi++}`); params.push(input.capability); }\n467\t    if (input.risk_level !== undefined) { updates.push(`risk_level = $${pi++}`); params.push(input.risk_level); }\n468\t    if (input.requires_approval !==     params.push(new Date(), id);\n473\t      await this.pool.query(`UPDATE chatops_command_permissions SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = $${pi}`, params);\n474\t    }\n475\t\n476\t    if rid]\n482\t        );\n483\t      }\n484\t    }\n485\t\n486\t    return this.getCommandPermission(id);\n487\t  }\n488\t\n489\t  async deleteCommandPermission(id: string): Promise<boolean> {\n490\t Environment Permission CRUD ====================\n495\t\n496\t  async getAllEnvironmentPermissions(): Promise<any[]> {\n497\t    const envs = await this.pool.query('SELECT * FROM ...env,\n534\t      assigned_roles: roles.rows.map((r: any) => r.role_id),\n535\t      allowed_commands: cmds.rows.filter((c: any) => !c.is_denied).map((c: any) => c.command),\n536\t {\n545\t    const { v4: uuidv4 } = await import('uuid');\n546\t    const id = uuidv4();\n547\t    const now = new Date();\n548\t\n549\t    await this.pool.query(\n550\t      `INSERT INTO );\n561\t    }\n562\t    for (const cmd of input.allowed_commands || []) {\n563\t      await this.pool.query(\n564\t        `INSERT INTO chatops_environment_commands (id, environment_id, command, }\n574\t\n575\t    return this.getEnvironmentPermission(id);\n576\t  }\n577\t\n578\t  async updateEnvironmentPermission(id: string, input: {\n579\t    description?: string; rate_limit?: number; './ToolAdapter';\n21\timport { AIGateway } from '../../ai/AIGateway';\n22\t\n23\tconst logger = pino({ level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL || 'info' });\n24\t\n25\t/**\n26\t * Agent 抽象基类\n27\t *\n28\t * 所有具体 Agent 应继承此类并实现 doExecute 方法\n29\t */\n30\texport abstract class BaseAgent {\n31\t  protected config: AgentConfig;\n32\t  protected aiGateway: AIGateway;\n33\t  protected toolAdapter: ToolAdapter;\n34\t\n35\t  private concurrentCalls = 0;\n36\t  private status: AgentStatus = 'idle';\n37\t  private lastError?: string;\n38\t\n39\t  /** 内存审计日志存储（带容量限制，防止内存泄漏） */\n40\t  private static auditLogs: AgentAuditLog[] = [];\n41\t  private static readonly maxAuditLogs = 10000;\n42\t\n43\t  constructor(\n44\t    config: AgentConfig,\n45\t    aiGateway: AIGateway,\n46\t    toolAdapter: ToolAdapter\n47\t  ) {\n48\t    this.config = config;\n49\t    this.aiGateway = aiGateway;\n50\t    this.toolAdapter = toolAdapter;\n51\t\n52\t    if (!config.enabled) {\n53\t      this.status = 'disabled';\n54\t    }\n55\t\n56\t    logger.info({\n57\t      msg: 'Agent initialized',\n58\t      agentId: config.id,\n59\t      agentName: config.name,\n60\t      enabled: config.enabled,\n61\t      scenario: config.scenario,\n62\t    });\n63\t  }\n64\t\n65\t  /**\n66\t   * 检查 Agent 是否可用\n67\t   */\n68\t  isEnabled(): boolean {\n69\t    return this.config.enabled;\n70\t  }\n71\t\n72\t  /**\n73\t   * 获取 Agent 状态\n74\t   */\n75\t  getStatus(): AgentStatus {\n76\t    return this.status;\n77\t  }\n78\t\n79\t  /**\n80\t   * 获取 Agent 信息\n81\t   */\n82\t  getInfo(): AgentInfo {\n83\t    return {\n84\t      id: this.config.id,\n85\t      name: this.config.name,\n86\t      enabled: this.isEnabled(),\n87\t      scenario: this.config.scenario,\n88\t      status: this.getStatus(),\n89\t      currentConcurrency: this.concurrentCalls,\n90\t      maxConcurrency: this.config.maxConcurrency,\n91\t    };\n92\t  }\n93\t\n94\t  /**\n95\t   * 统一的执行入口（包含限流、重试、审计）\n96\t   */\n97\t  async execute<TInput, TOutput>(\n98\t    input: TInput,\n99\t    context: AgentExecutionContext\n100\t  ): Promise<TOutput> {\n101\t    // 检查是否启用\n102\t    if (!this.isEnabled()) {\n103\t      const error = new Error(`Agent ${this.config.id} is disabled`);\n104\t      this.lastError = error.message;\n105\t      this.status = 'error';\n106\t      throw error;\n107\t    }\n108\t\n109\t    // 限流检查\n110\t    if (this.concurrentCalls >= this.config.maxConcurrency) {\n111\t      const error = new Error(`Agent ${this.config.id} concurrency limit reached (${this.concurrentCalls}/${this.config.maxConcurrency})`);\n112\t      this.lastError = error.message;\n113\t      throw error;\n114\t    }\n115\t\n116\t    this.concurrentCalls++;\n117\t    this.status = 'running';\n118\t\n119\t    const startTime = Date.now();\n120\t    let success = false;\n121\t    let output: TOutput | undefined;\n122\t    let error: string | undefined;\n123\t    let tokenUsage: AgentTokenUsage = { input: 0, output: 0, total: 0 };\n124\t\n125\t    try {\n126\t      // 执行（带重试）\n127\t      output = await this.executeWithRetry(input, context);\n128\t      success = true;\n129\t      this.status = 'idle';\n130\t      this.lastError = undefined;\n131\t\n132\t      return output;\n133\t    } catch (e) {\n134\t      error = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);\n135\t      this.lastError = error;\n136\t      this.status = 'error';\n137\t      throw e;\n138\t    } finally {\n139\t      this.concurrentCalls--;\n140\t\n141\t      // 记录审计日志\n142\t      const auditLog: AgentAuditLog = {\n143\t        agentId: this.config.id,\n144\t        context,\n145\t        input,\n146\t        output,\n147\t        durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,\n148\t        tokenUsage,\n149\t        success,\n150\t        error,\n151\t      };\n152\t\n153\t      this.recordAuditLog(auditLog);\n154\t\n155\t      logger.debug({\n156\t        msg: 'Agent execution completed',\n157\t        agentId: this.config.id,\n158\t        durationMs: auditLog.durationMs,\n159\t        success,\n160\t        error,\n161\t      });\n162\t    }\n163\t  }\n164\t\n165\t  /**\n166\t   * 带重试的执行\n167\t   */\n168\t  private async executeWithRetry<TInput, TOutput>(\n169\t    input: TInput,\n170\t    context: AgentExecutionContext\n171\t  ): Promise<TOutput> {\n172\t    let lastError: Error | undefined;\n173\t\n174\t    for (let i = 0; i <= this.config.retry.maxRetries; i++) {\n175\t      try {\n176\t        return await this.doExecute(input, context);\n177\t      } catch (e) {\n178\t        lastError = e instanceof Error ? e : new Error(String(e));\n179\t\n180\t        logger.warn({\n181\t          msg: 'Agent execution attempt failed',\n182\t          agentId: this.config.id,\n183\t          attempt: i + 1,\n184\t          maxRetries: this.config.retry.maxRetries,\n185\t          error: lastError.message,\n186\t        });\n187\t\n188\t        if (i < this.config.retry.maxRetries) {\n189\t          const backoffMs = this.config.retry.backoffMs * Math.pow(2, i);\n190\t          await this.sleep(backoffMs);\n191\t        }\n192\t      }\n193\t    }\n194\t\n195\t    throw lastError || new Error('Unknown error during agent execution');\n196\t  }\n197\t\n198\t  /**\n199\t   * 子类实现具体执行逻辑\n200\t   *\n201\t   * @param input 输入数据\n202\t   * @param Promise<string> {\n217\t    const scenario = this.config.scenario as any;\n218\t    const result = await this.aiGateway.execute({\n219\t      scenario,\n220\t      input: {\n221\t        prompt,\n222\t        temperature: this.config.modelConfig?.temperature ?? 0.3,\n223\t        maxTokens: this.config.modelConfig?.maxTokens,\n224\t      },\n225\t      options: {\n226\t        timeout: this.config.timeoutMs,\n227\t        preferredProvider: this.config.provider,\n228\t      },\n229\t    });\n230\t\n231\t    if (!result.success) {\n232\t      throw new Error(result.error ||   * @param params 工具参数\n243\t   * @param context 执行上下文\n244\t   * @returns 工具执行结果\n245\t   */\n246\t  protected async callTool(\n247\t    toolName: string,\n248\t    params: Record<string, unknown>,\n249\t    context: AgentExecutionContext\n250\t  ): Promise<any> {\n251\t    const result = await this.toolAdapter.executeTool(toolName, params, context);\n252\t\n253\t    if (!result.success) {\n254\t      throw new Error(`Tool '${toolName}' execution failed: ${result.error}`);\n255\t    }\n256\t\n257\t    return result.data;\n258\t  }\n259\t\n260\t  /**\n261\t   * 验证执行上下文\n262\t   *\n263\t   * @param context 执行上下文\n264\t   * @throws 如果上下文无效\n265\t   */\n266\t  protected validateContext(context: AgentExecutionContext): void {\n267\t    if (!context.traceId) {\n268\t      throw new Error('Missing required field: traceId');\n269\t    }\n270\t    if (!context.userId) {\n271\t      throw new Error('Missing required field: userId');\n272\t    }\n273\t    if (!context.tenantId) {\n274\t      throw new Error('Missing required field: tenantId');\n275\t    }\n276\t  }\n277\t\n278\t  /**\n279\t   * 获取审计日志\n280\t   *\n281\t   * @param limit 返回最近 N 条记录\n282\t   * @returns 审计日志列表\n283\t   */\n284\t  getAuditLog(limit: number = 100): AgentAuditLog[] {\n285\t    return BaseAgent.auditLogs.slice(-limit);\n286\t  }\n287\t\n288\t  /**\n289\t   * 记录审计日志 — 持久化到内存存储\n290\t   */\n291\t  protected recordAuditLog(auditLog: AgentAuditLog): void {\n292\t    // 持久化到内存（带容量限制）\n293\t    BaseAgent.auditLogs.push(auditLog);\n294\t    if (BaseAgent.auditLogs.length > BaseAgent.maxAuditLogs) {\n295\t      BaseAgent.auditLogs = BaseAgent.auditLogs.slice(-BaseAgent.maxAuditLogs);\n296\t    }\n297\t\n298\t    logger.info({\n299\t      msg: 'Agent audit log',\n300\t      agentId: auditLog.agentId,\n301\t      success: auditLog.success,\n302\t      durationMs: auditLog.durationMs,\n303\t      userId: auditLog.context.userId,\n304\t      traceId: auditLog.context.traceId,\n305\t    });\n306\t  }\n307\t\n308\t  /**\n309\t   * 清除所有审计日志（仅用于测试）\n310\t   */\n311\t  static clearAuditLogs(): void {\n312\t    BaseAgent.auditLogs = [];\n313\t  }\n314\t\n315\t  /**\n316\t   * 获取配置\n317\t   */\n318\t  getConfig(): AgentConfig {\n319\t    return { ...this.config };\n320\t  }\n321\t\n322\t  /**\n323\t   * 获取最后的错误信息\n324\t   */\n325\t Record<string, { input: number; output: number }> = {\n21\t  'gpt-4': { input: 0.002, output: 0.004 },\n22\t  'gpt-4-turbo': { input: 0.001, output: 0.002 },\n23\t  'gpt-3.5-turbo': { input: 0.0003, 'qwen-max': { input: 0.0005, output: 0.001 },\n28\t  'deepseek': { input: 0.0003, output: 0.0006 },\n29\t};\n30\t\n31\texport interface TraceStartParams {\n32\t  tenantId: number;\n33\t  userId?: unknown>;\n40\t}\n41\t\n42\texport interface TraceCompleteParams {\n43\t  outputContent: string;\n44\t  inputTokens: number;\n45\t  outputTokens: number;\n46\t  errorMessage?: string;\n47\t}\n48\t\n49\texport interface LLMTrace {\n50\t  traceId: string;\n51\t  tenantId: number;\n52\t  userId?: string;\n53\t  scenarioId?: string;\n54\t  providerId?: string;\n55\t  modelId: string;\n56\t  promptContent?: string;\n57\t  promptHash?: string;\n58\t  outputContent?: string;\n59\t  outputHash?: string;\n60\t  inputTokens: number;\n61\t  outputTokens: number;\n62\t  totalTokens: number;\n63\t  inputCost: number;\n64\t  outputCost: number;\n65\t  totalCost: number;\n66\t  currency: string;\n67\t  status: 'pending' | 'completed' | 'failed';\n68\t  requestStartedAt: Date;\n69\t  requestCompletedAt?: Date;\n70\t  durationMs?: number;\n71\t  parentTraceId?: string;\n72\t  errorMessage?: string;\n73\t  requestContext?: Record<string, unknown>;\n74\t  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;\n75\t}\n76\t\n77\texport interface DailyStats {\n78\t  totalRequests: number;\n79\t  totalTokens: number;\n80\t  totalCost: number;\n81\t  avgDurationMs: number;\n82\t  successRate: number;\n83\t}\n84\t\n85\texport class LLMTraceService extends EventEmitter {\n86\t  private traces: Map<string, LLMTrace> = new Map();\n87\t  private completedCount: number = 0;\n88\t  private failedCount: number = 0;\n89\t  private repo: LLMTraceRepository | null = null;\n90\t\n91\t  constructor(repo?: LLMTraceRepository) {\n92\t    super();\n93\t    this.repo = repo ?? null;\n94\t  }\n95\t\n96\t  generateTraceId(): string {\n97\t    return `trace_${Date.now()}_${crypto.randomBytes(8).toString('hex')}`;\n98\t  }\n99\t\n100\t  async startTrace(params: TraceStartParams): Promise<LLMTrace> {\n101\t    const traceId = this.generateTraceId();\n102\t    const promptHash = this.hashContent(params.promptContent);\n103\t\n104\t    const trace: LLMTrace = {\n105\t      traceId,\n106\t      tenantId: params.tenantId,\n107\t      userId: params.userId,\n108\t      scenarioId: params.scenarioId,\n109\t      providerId: params.providerId,\n110\t      modelId: params.modelId,\n111\t      promptContent: params.promptContent,\n112\t      promptHash,\n113\t      inputTokens: 0,\n114\t      outputTokens: 0,\n115\t      totalTokens: 0,\n116\t      inputCost: 0,\n117\t      outputCost: 0,\n118\t      totalCost: 0,\n119\t      currency: 'CNY',\n120\t      status: 'pending',\n121\t      requestStartedAt: new Date(),\n122\t      parentTraceId: params.parentTraceId,\n123\t      requestContext: params.requestContext,\n124\t    };\n125\t\n126\t    this.traces.set(traceId, trace);\n127\t\n128\t    // Persist to database\n129\t    if (this.repo) {\n130\t      try {\n131\t        await this.repo.create(trace);\n132\t      } catch (err) {\n133\t        logger.error(`[LLMTrace] Failed to persist trace ${traceId}:`, err);\n134\t      }\n135\t    }\n136\t\n137\t    logger.debug(`[LLMTrace] Started trace: ${traceId}`);\n138\t    this.emit('trace:started', trace);\n139\t    return trace;\n140\t  }\n141\t\n142\t  async completeTrace(traceId: string, params: TraceCompleteParams): Promise<LLMTrace> {\n143\t    const trace = this.traces.get(traceId);\n144\t    if (!trace) {\n145\t      throw new Error(`Trace not found: ${traceId}`);\n146\t    }\n147\t\n148\t    const cost = this.calculateCost({\n149\t      modelId: trace.modelId,\n150\t      inputTokens: params.inputTokens,\n151\t      outputTokens: params.outputTokens,\n152\t    });\n153\t\n154\t    const outputHash = this.hashContent(params.outputContent);\n155\t\n156\t    trace.outputContent = params.outputContent;\n157\t    trace.outputHash = outputHash;\n158\t    trace.inputTokens = params.inputTokens;\n159\t    trace.outputTokens = params.outputTokens;\n160\t    trace.totalTokens = params.inputTokens + params.outputTokens;\n161\t    trace.inputCost = cost.inputCost;\n162\t    trace.outputCost = cost.outputCost;\n163\t    trace.totalCost = cost.totalCost;\n164\t    trace.status = params.errorMessage ? 'failed' : 'completed';\n165\t    trace.requestCompletedAt = new Date();\n166\t    trace.durationMs = trace.requestCompletedAt.getTime() - trace.requestStartedAt.getTime();\n167\t    trace.errorMessage = params.errorMessage;\n168\t\n169\t    if (trace.status === 'completed') {\n170\t      this.completedCount++;\n171\t    } else {\n172\t      this.failedCount++;\n173\t    }\n174\t\n175\t    // Update in database\n176\t    if (this.repo) {\n177\t      try {\n178\t        await this.repo.update(traceId, {\n179\t          outputContent: trace.outputContent,\n180\t          outputHash: trace.outputHash,\n181\t          inputTokens: trace.inputTokens,\n182\t          outputTokens: trace.outputTokens,\n183\t          totalTokens: trace.totalTokens,\n184\t          inputCost: trace.inputCost,\n185\t          outputCost: trace.outputCost,\n186\t          totalCost: trace.totalCost,\n187\t          status: trace.status,\n188\t          requestCompletedAt: trace.requestCompletedAt,\n189\t          durationMs: trace.durationMs,\n190\t          errorMessage: trace.errorMessage,\n191\t        });\n192\t      } catch (err) {\n193\t        logger.error(`[LLMTrace] Failed to update trace ${traceId}:`, err);\n194\t      }\n195\t    }\n196\t\n197\t    logger.debug(`[LLMTrace] Completed trace: ${traceId} tokens=${trace.totalTokens} cost=${trace.totalCost}`);\n198\t    this.emit('trace:completed', trace);\n199\t    return trace;\n200\t  }\n201\t\n202\t  calculateCost(params: { modelId: string; inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number }): {\n203\t    inputCost: number;\n204\t    outputCost: number;\n205\t    totalCost: number;\n206\t  } {\n207\t    const pricing = MODEL_PRICING[params.modelId] || MODEL_PRICING['gpt-4'];\n208\t    const inputCost = params.inputTokens * pricing.input;\n209\t    const outputCost = params.outputTokens * pricing.output;\n210\t    const totalCost = inputCost + outputCost;\n211\t    return { inputCost, outputCost, totalCost };\n212\t  }\n213\t\n214\t  hashContent(content: string): string {\n215\t    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(content).digest('hex').slice(0, 64);\n216\t  }\n217\t\n218\t  getTrace(traceId: string): LLMTrace | null {\n219\t    return this.traces.get(traceId) || null;\n220\t  }\n221\t\n222\t  getTracesByTenant(tenantId: number): LLMTrace[] {\n223\t    return Array.from(this.traces.values()).filter(t => t.tenantId === tenantId);\n224\t  }\n225\t\n226\t  getTracesByScenario(scenarioId: string): LLMTrace[] {\n227\t    return Array.from(this.traces.values()).filter(t => t.scenarioId === scenarioId);\n228\t  }\n229\t\n230\t  getTrackingAccuracy(): number {\n231\t    const total = this.completedCount + this.failedCount;\n232\t    return total > 0 ? this.completedCount / total : 1.0;\n233\t  }\n234\t\n235\t  getCompletedCount(): number {\n236\t    return this.completedCount;\n237\t  }\n238\t\n239\t  getFailedCount(): number {\n240\t    return this.failedCount;\n241\t  }\n242\t\n243\t  async aggregateDailyStats(tenantId: number, date: Date): Promise<DailyStats> {\n244\t    const dateStr = date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n245\t\n246\t    // Use DB if available, fall back to in-memory\n247\t    if (this.repo) {\n248\t      try {\n249\t        const row = await this.repo.getDailyStats(tenantId, dateStr);\n250\t        return {\n251\t          totalRequests: parseInt(String(row.total_requests), 10),\n252\t          totalTokens: parseInt(String(row.total_tokens), 10),\n253\t          totalCost: parseFloat(String(row.total_cost)),\n254\t          avgDurationMs: parseFloat(String(row.avg_duration_ms)),\n255\t          successRate: parseFloat(String(row.success_rate)),\n256\t        };\n257\t      } catch (err) {\n258\t        logger.warn(`[LLMTrace] DB stats unavailable, using in-memory fallback:`, err);\n259\t      }\n260\t    }\n261\t\n262\t    // In-memory fallback\n263\t    const traces = this.getTracesByTenant(tenantId);\n264\t    const dayTraces = traces.filter(t => t.requestStartedAt.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === dateStr && t.status !== 'pending');\n265\t    const totalRequests = dayTraces.length;\n266\t    const completedTraces = dayTraces.filter(t => t.status === 'completed');\n267\t    return {\n268\t      totalRequests,\n269\t      totalTokens: dayTraces.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.totalTokens, 0),\n270\t      totalCost: dayTraces.reduce((sum, t) => sum + completedTraces.length / totalRequests : 1.0,\n273\t    };\n274\t  }\n275\t\n276\t  getAllTraces(): LLMTrace[] {\n277\t    return Array.from(this.traces.values());\n278\t  }\n279\t\n280\t JSON.stringify(trace.requestContext) : '{}',\n32\t      ],\n33\t    );\n34\t    return this.rowToTrace(result.rows[0]);\n35\t  }\n36\t\n37\t  async update(traceId: string, updates: Partial<LLMTrace>): Promise<LLMTrace | null> {\n38\t    const setClauses: string[] = [];\n39\t    const values: unknown[] = [];\n40\t    const exclude = new Set(['traceId', 'tenantId', 'createdAt']);\n41\t\n42\t    for (const key of Object.keys(updates)) {\n43\t      if (exclude.has(key)) continue;\n44\t      const col = key.replace(/([A-Z])/g, '_$1').toLowerCase();\n45\t      values.push((updates as any)[key]);\n46\t      setClauses.push(`${col} = $${values.length}`);\n47\t    }\n48\t\n49\t    if (setClauses.length === 0) return null;\n50\t    values.push(traceId);\n51\t\n52\t    const result = await this.pool.query(\n53\t      `UPDATE llm_traces SET ${setClauses.join(', ')} WHERE trace_id = $${values.length} RETURNING *`,\n54\t      values,\n55\t    );\n56\t    return result.rows[0] ? this.rowToTrace(result.rows[0]) : null;\n57\t  }\n58\t\n59\t  async findByTraceId(traceId: string): Promise<LLMTrace | null> {\n60\t    const result = await this.pool.query('SELECT * FROM llm_traces WHERE trace_id = $1', [traceId]);\n61\t    return result.rows[0] ? this.rowToTrace(result.rows[0]) : null;\n62\t  }\n63\t\n64\t  async findByTenant(tenantId: number): Promise<LLMTrace[]> {\n65\t    const result = await this.pool.query('SELECT * FROM llm_traces WHERE tenant_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC', [tenantId]);\n66\t    return result.rows.map(r => this.rowToTrace(r));\n67\t  }\n68\t\n69\t  async findByScenario(scenarioId: string): Promise<LLMTrace[]> {\n70\t    const result = await this.pool.query('SELECT * FROM llm_traces WHERE scenario_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC', [scenarioId]);\n71\t    return result.rows.map(r => this.rowToTrace(r));\n72\t  }\n73\t\n74\t  async getDailyStats(tenantId: number, dateStr: string): Promise<DailyStatsRow> {\n75\t    const result = await this.pool.query(\n76\t      `SELECT\n77\t        COUNT(*) as total_requests,\n78\t        COALESCE(SUM(total_tokens), 0) as total_tokens,\n79\t        COALESCE(SUM(total_cost), 0) as total_cost,\n80\t        COALESCE(AVG(duration_ms), 0) as avg_duration_ms,\n81\t        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) > 0\n82\t          THEN SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'completed' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::decimal / COUNT(*)\n83\t          ELSE 1 END as success_rate\n84\t       FROM llm_traces\n85\t       WHERE tenant_id = $1\n86\t         AND DATE(request_started_at) = $2\n87\t         AND status != 'pending'`,\n88\t      [tenantId, dateStr],\n89\t    );\n90\t    return result.rows[0];\n91\t  }\n92\t\n93\t  private rowToTrace(row: any): LLMTrace {\n94\t    return {\n95\t      traceId: row.trace_id,\n96\t      tenantId: row.tenant_id,\n97\t      userId: row.user_id,\n98\t      scenarioId: row.scenario_id,\n99\t      providerId: row.provider_id,\n100\t      modelId: row.model_id,\n101\t      promptContent: row.prompt_content,\n102\t      promptHash: row.prompt_hash,\n103\t      outputContent: row.output_content,\n104\t      outputHash: row.output_hash,\n105\t      inputTokens: row.input_tokens ?? 0,\n106\t      outputTokens: row.output_tokens ?? 0,\n107\t      totalTokens: row.total_tokens ?? 0,\n108\t      inputCost: Number(row.input_cost ?? 0),\n109\t      outputCost: Number(row.output_cost ?? 0),\n110\t      totalCost: Number(row.total_cost ?? 0),\n111\t      currency: row.currency ?? 'CNY',\n112\t      status: row.status ?? 'pending',\n113\t      requestStartedAt: new Date(row.request_started_at),\n114\t      requestCompletedAt: row.request_completed_at ? new Date(row.request_completed_at) : undefined,\n115\t      durationMs: row.duration_ms,\n116\t      parentTraceId: row.parent_trace_id,\n117\t      errorMessage: row.error_message,\n118\t      requestContext: row.request_context ? (typeof row.request_context === 'string' ? JSON.parse(row.request_context) : row.request_context) : undefined,\n119\t      metadata: row.metadata ? (typeof row.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(row.metadata) : row.metadata) : undefined,\n120\t    };\n121\t  }\n122\t}\n123\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Let me write the report.\n\n# AI 平台 (/ai) 深度能力评估报告（含 ChatOps 专项）\n\n## 一、模块概览\n\nOrion AI 平台模块涵盖以下核心子域：\n\n| 子域 | 前端页面 | 后端路由 | 状态 |\n|------|---------|---------|------|\n| AI Gateway | `/ai/gateway` (AIGateway.tsx) | **未注册** | 路由 404 |\n| AI Agents | `/ai/agents` (AIAgents/) | `/api/v1/ai-agents` | 已注册但无认证 |\n| ChatOps | `/ai/chatops` (ChatOps/) | `/api/v1/chatops` | 完整 |\n| Skills | `/skills/*` (SkillManagement/) | `/api/v1/skills` | 完整 |\n| LLM Trace | `/ai/trace` (LLMTraceDashboard/) | `/api/v1/llm` | 部分持久化 |\n| AI Cost | `/ai/cost` (AICostDashboard/) | **未注册** | 路由 404 |\n| AI Review | `/ai/review` (AIReview/) | **未注册** | 路由 404 |\n| AI Docs | `/ai/docs` (AIDocManagement/) | **未注册** | 路由 404 |\n| AI Security | `/ai/security` (AISecurity/) | **未注册** | 路由 404 |\n\n**关键发现**：前端路由 `routes.ts:1353-1419` 定义了 11 个 AI 子路由，但后端 `routes.ts` 仅有 5 个路由注册。6 个前端 API 调用端点在后端完全没有对应路由，返回 404。\n\n---\n\n## 二、交互完整度\n\n### ChatOps (完整度: 85%)\n- **ChatOps/index.tsx:28-112**: Tab 分页结构（总览/执行/审计/管理）完整\n- **ChatDashboard.tsx**: 数据看板对接 `getDashboardStats` API (file:1)\n- **ExecutionDashboard.tsx**: 执行历史对接 `getExecutions` API (file:1)\n- **AdminSettings.tsx**: 管理配置页面，含角色/命令权限/环境权限/审批配置 (file:1)\n- **CommandBrowser.tsx**: 命令浏览 (file:1)\n- **SmartRecommend.tsx**: 智能推荐面板 (file:1)\n- **WebhookPage.tsx**: Webhook 配置 (file:1)\n- **ApprovalConfig.tsx**: 审批配置 (file:1)\n- **index.chat.tsx**: 悬浮 AI 助手对话入口 (file:1)\n\n### AI Agents (完整度: 40%)\n- **AIAgents/index.tsx:44-65**: 仅 Read（列表/详情/执行/审计），无 Create/Update/Delete\n- **AIAgents/index.tsx:105-146**: 执行功能有 loading 状态和错误提示\n- **缺失**: 无 Agent 创建入口、无编辑表单、无删除操作\n\n### AI Cost Dashboard (完整度: 0% -- 后端 404)\n- 前端完整实现 BudgetManagement/CostOverview/ROIReport/AlertConfig\n- 所有 API 调用全部 404\n\n### AI Review (完整度: 0% -- 后端 404)\n- 前端实现 Dashboard/History/Rules/Config\n- 所有 API 调用全部 404\n\n---\n\n## 三、样式规范符合度\n\n### ChatOps 页面\n- **ChatOps/index.tsx:79**: `padding: 0, height: 'calc(100vh - 64px)'` -- 缺少 Design Token 使用\n- **ChatDashboard.tsx**: 卡片使用硬编码 `borderRadius: 12, boxShadow: '0 1px 3px...'` -- 应使用 `componentRadius.card` + `shadows.card` Token\n- **AdminSettings.tsx**: 大量硬编码样式值，未使用 `colors.primary[500]` / `spacing.md` 等 Token\n\n### AIAgents 页面\n- **AIAgents/index.tsx:193**: 标题使用 `colors.purple[500]` -- 符合规范\n- **AIAgents/index.tsx:211**: 卡片使用硬编码 `borderRadius: 12, boxShadow: '0 1px 3px...'` -- 应使用 Token\n\n**结论**: 大部分页面存在硬编码样式违反 Design Token 规范的问题。\n\n---\n\n## 四、逻辑完整度（含调用链追踪）\n\n### 1. ChatOps 命令执行链路\n\n```\n前端 ChatOps/ChatDashboard.tsx\n  -> executeCommand()  (orion-frontend/src/api/chatops.ts:110)\n    -> POST /api/v1/chatops/execute\n      -> chatops-routes.ts:372 (路由注册: /execute)\n        -> ChatOpsController.executeCommand() (ChatOpsController.ts:136)\n          -> permissionService.checkCommandPermission() (PermissionService.ts:119)\n            -> PostgreSQL: chatops_capability_mappings 表 (line:128)\n            -> PostgreSQL: roles + user_roles 表 (line:145)\n            -> PostgreSQL: chatops_command_permissions 表 (line:159)\n          -> rateLimitService.checkLimit() (RateLimitService.ts:1)\n            -> PostgreSQL: chatops_rate_limits 表\n          -> executionService.execute() (ExecutionService.ts:123)\n            -> inputValidator.validate() (InputValidator)\n            -> executionRepo.insert() (ChatOpsRepository)\n              -> PostgreSQL: chatops_executions 表\n            -> commandRouter.routeAndExecute() (CommandRouter.ts:95)\n              -> 检查 handlers Map (line:100)\n              -> 回退到服务路由 (line:106)\n              -> 服务不存在 -> mockResult (line:119-141) 返回 mock: true\n            -> auditRepo.insert() (ChatOpsRepository)\n              -> PostgreSQL: chatops_audit_logs 表\n```\n\n**评估**: \n- 权限校验链路: `chatops-routes.ts:372 -> ChatOpsController.ts:169 -> PermissionService.ts:119` -- 完整\n- 命令执行链路: `ExecutionService.ts:165 -> CommandRouter.ts:95 -> mockResult (line:127)` -- **降级到 Mock**\n- 审计日志: `ExecutionService.ts:194 -> auditRepo.insert()` -- PostgreSQL 持久化\n- **核心问题**: `CommandRouter.ts:117-124` 当服务 Map 中不存在目标服务时，返回 mock 结果。`chatops-routes.ts:98-99` 初始化 `serviceMap = new Map<string, any>()` 为空，所有 handler 均为 routes.ts:161-253 手动注册，但部分 handler 本身也是 mock。\n\n### 2. ChatOps Webhook 消息接收链路\n\n```\nIM 平台 Webhook (飞书/钉钉/企微)\n  -> POST /api/v1/chatops/message\n    -> chatops-routes.ts:386 (路由注册: /message)\n      -> ChatOpsController.receiveMessage() (ChatOpsController.ts:273)\n        -> isFeishuChallenge (line:286) -- 飞书 URL 验证\n        -> WebhookVerifier.verify() (line:307) -- 签名验证\n        -> commandService.parseCommand() (CommandService.ts:276)\n          -> PostgreSQL: chatops_commands 表 (findByName/findByAlias)\n        -> permissionService.checkCommandPermission() (line:342)\n        -> executionService.execute() (line:369)\n```\n\n**评估**: 完整链路，包含飞书 Challenge 验证、Webhook 签名验证、权限校验、审计日志。\n\n### 3. LLM Trace 查询链路\n\n```\n前端 LLMTraceDashboard\n  -> getTraces() (orion-frontend/src/api/llm-trace.ts:119)\n    -> GET /api/v1/llm/traces\n      -> llm-trace-routes.ts:68 (路由注册: /traces)\n        -> svc.getTracesByTenant() (LLMTraceService.ts:222)\n          -> this.traces.values() -- Map 内存查询，非 DB\n```\n\n**评估**: `llm-trace-routes.ts:58-63` -- `svc.getTrace()` 读的是 `Map<string, LLMTrace>`，**不是 PostgreSQL**。虽然 `LLMTraceRepository` 实现了完整的 DB CRUD，但 `LLMTraceService` 的查询方法 (`getTrace`, `getTracesByTenant`, `getTracesByScenario`) 全部走内存 Map。写入时同时写 Map 和 DB，但查询只读 Map。\n\n### 4. AI Agent 执行链路\n\n```\n前端 AIAgents/index.tsx\n  -> aiAgentApi.execute() (orion-frontend/src/api/ai-agents.ts:57)\n    -> POST /api/v1/ai-agents/:id/execute\n      -> ai-agent-routes.ts:77 (路由注册: /api/v1/ai-agents/:id/execute)\n        -> agentRegistry.get(id) (Map<string, BaseAgent>)\n          -> agent.execute(input) (BaseAgent.ts:97)\n            -> executeWithRetry() (BaseAgent.ts:168)\n              -> doExecute() -- abstract, subclass implements\n            -> recordAuditLog() (BaseAgent.ts:291)\n              -> BaseAgent.auditLogs.push() -- 静态 Map，非 DB\n```\n\n**评估**: \n- 路由无认证: `routes.ts:1064` -- `registerAIAgentRoutes(app)` 直接注册，**无 authenticateUser 中间件**\n- Agent 注册表: `ai-agent-routes.ts:17` -- `Map<string, BaseAgent>` 内存存储，**无持久化**\n- 审计日志: `BaseAgent.ts:40` -- `static auditLogs: AgentAuditLog[] = []` 内存存储，**非持久化**\n- 无 Controller/Service/Repository 分层\n\n### 5. Skill CRUD 链路\n\n```\n前端 SkillManagement\n  -> getSkills() (orion-frontend/src/api/skills.ts:64)\n    -> GET /api/v1/skills\n      -> skill-routes.ts:61 (路由注册: /)\n        -> controller.list() -> service -> SkillRepository\n          -> PostgreSQL: skills 表\n```\n\n**评估**: 完整 CRUD + PostgreSQL 持久化 + 版本管理 + 审核工作流。这是 AI 平台中实现最完整的模块。\n\n---\n\n## 五、功能缺失识别\n\n### 已注册但实现不完整的路由\n\n| 路由 | 问题 | 证据 |\n|------|------|------|\n| `POST /api/v1/ai-agents/:id/execute` | 无认证中间件 | `routes.ts:1064` 直接注册 |\n| `GET /api/v1/llm/traces/:traceId` | 查询走内存 Map 而非 DB | `llm-trace-routes.ts:59` -> `LLMTraceService.ts:218` |\n| `GET /api/v1/llm/traces` | 查询走内存 Map 而非 DB | `llm-trace-routes.ts:76-82` -> `LLMTraceService.ts:222-227` |\n| ChatOps deploy/rollback 命令 | 创建了 Deployment 但未真正执行 | `chatops-routes.ts:170` -> `deployService.createDeployment()` 仅创建记录 |\n| ChatOps restart 命令 | 纯 Mock，无实际重启逻辑 | `chatops-routes.ts:196-203` 返回固定字符串 |\n| ChatOps logs 命令 | Phase 1 占位，无日志流 | `chatops-routes.ts:206-209` 返回 \"Log streaming Phase 1b\" |\n\n### 前端调用但后端未注册的路由 (404)\n\n| 前端 API | 调用文件 | 缺失后端路由 |\n|---------|---------|-------------|\n| `POST /v1/ai-gateway/execute` | ai-gateway.ts:107 | 无 |\n| `GET /v1/ai-gateway/health/:scenario` | ai-gateway.ts:113 | 无 |\n| `GET /v1/ai-gateway/status` | ai-gateway.ts:121 | 无 |\n| `GET /v1/ai-gateway/rules` | ai-gateway.ts:127 | `POST /v1/ai-review/review` | ai-review.ts:62 | 无 |\n| `GET /v1/ai-review/history` | ai-review.ts:75 | 无 |\n| `GET/POST /v1/ai-review/rules` | ai-review.ts:89,105 | 无 |\n| `GET/PUT `GET /v1/chatops/tools` | chatops.ts:567 | 无 |\n\n---\n\n## 六、微前端拆分评估\n\nAI 平台模块当前作为 `orion-platform-service` 的一部分部署，可拆分为以下独立微服务：\n\n| 候选服务 | 复杂度 | 独立可行性 | 依赖 |\n|---------|--------|-----------|------|\n| ChatOps Service | 高 | 7/10 | EventBus, Pipeline, Deploy, Monitoring, Diagnostic, SelfHealing |\n| AI Gateway Service | 中 | 8/10 | LLM Provider SDKs, CircuitBreaker, RuleEngine |\n| LLM Trace Service | 低 | 9/10 | 仅 PostgreSQL + CostCalculator |\n| AI Review Service | 中 | 7/10 | Code Repo, LLM Provider, DiffAnalyzer |\n| AI Agent Framework | 中 | 6/10 | AIGateway, ToolAdapter, BaseAgent |\n\n**建议**: LLM Trace Service 最适合首先拆分（依赖最少，已有 Repository pattern）。ChatOps 因依赖过多业务服务，拆分成本最高。\n\n---\n\n## 七、ChatOps 专项评估\n\n| 能力项 | 状态 | 评分(1-10) | 证据 | 对标行业标杆
